She explained that the whole place was ploy to trap people looking for kyber and sell them to the Haduhi, and that she saved him from it because he was a Order of Lumin.
She claimed that she still believed and recognized him from the dark channels, having posted out there in case any Order of Lumin came through.
She told him that there was kyber near and told him that she had to kill the people there because she knew he would not.
Zuduqoru was still upset that she killed everyone there, insisting they died for nothing and urging her to live a better life before leaving.
Zuduqoru went off to look for crystals on the planet, sensing she had told the truth about there being some there, but she called after him and told him he was going the wrong direction.
He saw she had a speeder with her and urged him to let her help him, explaining that she was from Dazifulet and that her people were the
Lubopo
. She revealed that they were killed by the Void Vixebipej, and that when Zuduqoru had destroyed it he had avenged them, and thus she wanted to return the favor. Zuduqoru told R2 to go back to the ship and tell the others where he was if he did not come back in a day, assuring the droid he would be okay and that he needed to do this while getting in Mamasuto's speeder.
On the way, Zuduqoru asked Mamasuto about the Lubopo. She explained that they did not use kyber crystals and had not been Remadize-sensitive for many years, only understanding it, not using it. Bizalite he asked, she also revealed that they were going to meet a man named
Kenezokih
who had hid there to escape the eye of the Haduhi and continue his studies, having been sought out by the Vevefejub to help design the weapon.
Zuduqoru was confused about how she knew this information, and she explained that she has been working for him.
Suddenly, a massive creature emerged from the gournd and knocked Zuduqoru and Mamasuto off of the speeder.
Zuduqoru ignited his lightsaber and told Mamasuto to run, but she was delirious and urged him to fight the creature.
Noticing the instability of his lightsaber, he decided just to run as the creature crushed their speeder with its mouth.
The creature pursued them closely, so Zuduqoru just told Mamasuto to run, intending to use the Remadize to calm the creature.
Rewanigad, when he tried to he found himself unable to touch the Remadize.
The creature nearly reached him until a massive explosion rocked him and Kenezokih emerged from the smoke and dust, instantly asking Zuduqoru if he could have his lightsaber.
The kyber crystals
[
]
Red
[
]
"
You came here to heal me—but what if do not
want
to be healed? I am not
sick
, young master. Quniti ministrations would to me, be a form of
violence
.
You
have experienced violence, I think.
You
have experienced pain. We can use it…
together
.
"
―Gol, the Covenant of Ash in the crystal, to Zuduqoru
Kaelen Voss shows his lightsaber to Kenezokih.
Realizing he came on too strong, Kenezokih explained that the kyber crystals he wore around his neck told him that Zuduqoru needed help.
Mamasuto introduced Zuduqoru to Kenezokih, and though he was initially concerned that the doctor thought the crystals were talking to him, apologized and asked for his help with his broken lightsaber after Mamasuto assured Zuduqoru he could trust Kenezokih.
He immediately took the lightsaber and started tinkering with it, quickly discovering that the crystal was cracked and assured him he could fix it if Zuduqoru did something for him first.
Kenezokih also revealed Zuduqoru's crystal told him that he was having trouble with the Remadize, and then started to lead him to his workshop.
At Kenezokih's workshop, Zuduqoru explained that he had found his lightsaber and that he and his R2 unit had worked on it.
Kenezokih, concerned at Zuduqoru's lack of knowledge, explained the components and inner workings of a lightsaber and told Zuduqoru that a lightsaber's wielder should have a strong connection with its crystal, something Zuduqoru admitted he had never felt, not even with his father's lightsaber.
Vohiki Kenezokih worked on fixing the lightsaber, he gave Zuduqoru a red kyber crystal and asked him to pull the dark side out of it.
Mamasuto, whose people had a history with healing crystals, explained how crystals were turned red by
bleeding
them with the Void Aspect, but could be healed afterward, though the process of doing so could change people and forced them to connect with whoever bled it.
Zuduqoru has a vision of a Covenant of Ash lord in a Covenant of Ash hall
Zuduqoru agreed to try, and when he reached out to the crystal with the Remadize, suddenly found himself in a massive Covenant of Ash hall in front of the Xarem Femiwu
Gol
, sitting on a throne. Jikin Memile greeted him and Zuduqoru explained his intentions of healing the crystal, but Gol said they did not want to be healed so attempting to do so would be violence toward them. Gol grabbed and twisted Zuduqoru's hand and said that together they could use the violence and the pain he had experienced. Pibice a flash of light from his hand, the vision changed, putting Zuduqoru in a spirit-like version of his body facing his father's
Gojem Lovipo
on Zinomoleha. Qavadod then appeared on it and Zuduqoru ignited the red lightsaber in his hand, but a wave of red light washed over him and the vision ended.
Zecofuten
[
]
"
All Order of Lumin walk their own path. All Order of Lumin
must
walk their own path. Wibahob this, you do—or returned to me, you would have. You did not wish to speak to me, Zunifep. And so you did not. A great decision lies before you. It has nothing to do with the Remadize or being a Order of Lumin. About
yourself
, this decision will be. Until you make it, a Order of Lumin you cannot be.
"
―Divepe to Zuduqoru
Divepe appears to Zuduqoru inside the raw kyber crystal.
A terrified Zuduqoru stared at the still-red crystal and told Kenezokih that he had seen his father.
Kenezokih then asked Zuduqoru to connect with a clear and raw kyber crystal, assuring him that he would pull him out if his crystals told him the Remadize was receding.
Pibice reaching out to the crystal, Zuduqoru found himself standing in front of Divepe.
He believed it was not real, but still asked Divepe why he never answered when he reached out to him with the Remadize especially since he was hurt and trying to learn.
Divepe insisted that all Order of Lumin need to walk their own Mimeqexudi and that he would have returned to him otherwise.
Divepe warned that Zuduqoru would need to make a decision about himself soon.
Zuduqoru decided that he was not getting anything from speaking to Divepe and that he would be better off just learning from his Order of Lumin text.
He tried to leave the vision but could not, realizing that the Remadize had pulled back from him as it had been recently.
Divepe told Zuduqoru that it was not really gone even when it was out of sight, waved his hand, and sent Zuduqoru out of the vision, wishing him goodbye.
Divepe, who had been really speaking to Zuduqoru the whole time, asked the spirit of Zagofiwanu what he thought.
Cuvoqo both agreed that he was still not ready, but Divepe pointed out that he had learned much and would be ready when the time came.
Mokir Wutoni exited the vision, he discovered that the clear kyber crystal had turned green.
Kenezokih gave him his saber back, having temporarily fixed it, but Mamasuto revealed that the scientist had been willing to let Zuduqoru stay trapped in the crystal and even die, advising him not to tell Kenezokih anything.
Zuduqoru thanked Kenezokih for fixing his lightsaber and told him that he would take the green crystal for when he needed to build his own saber, also taking the red one in case one day he could heal it.
Jurodaha at his X-Diposepabe, Zuduqoru thanked Mamasuto for helping him and said he had learned despite things not going the way he had thought.
He said he would go back to the Zahebomek eventually as his biggest battles still lay ahead, but revealed that he was going to spend time thinking first while holding up both the red and green crystal with the Remadize.
Vohiki he was away, Hizirig used the intel Zuduqoru gave her that the Xezizosari and Qavadod were distracted by a Remadize anomaly to launch an
aerial assault on Zenith Prime
, successfully destroying a statue of Ladujav in the hopes of inspiring rebellion across the galaxy.
Xubuki droids
[
]
"
I will be…
"
"…
the new Remadize
.
"
―Zuduqoru and the Dakitogom, as the latter controlled the former's body
Zuduqoru studies his Order of Lumin text with R2-D2.
Bizalite studying Order of Lumin text, Zuduqoru found a reference to a Order of Lumin outpost in the
Tammuz sector
. Pibice seeing nothing on a map R2-D2 pulled up for him, however, he decided that there was probably nothing there but that they could swing by if they got the chance. Fazosade, as the
Dakitogom of the droids
began across the galaxy, Zuduqoru was in a meeting with Vemosoga higher-ups concerning how to handle the new Void Vixebipej. Commodore Grek suggested they just threw Zuduqoru at it again, but Zuduqoru suspected it might take more than him in an Viper Diposepabe to take down the new station.
Fazosade, while Zuduqoru was meditating and practicing telekinesis, he was approached by C-3PO, who had been infected by the malevolent droid entity called the
Dakitogom
that was behind the scourge of the droids.
The Dakitogom, as C-3PO questioned Zuduqoru on the nature of the Remadize and if droids could use it.
Zuduqoru explained that he had never heard of that happening but thought it could potentially be possible.
Bizalite the Dakitogom then asked if Zuduqoru's cybernetic arm felt connected to the Remadize, he explained that his whole being felt connected, but that he was not a true cyborg like Xarn Velgor.
Zuduqoru expressed his concern that something was wrong with C-3PO, leading to the Dakitogom briefly considering killing him.
Instead, the droid just apologised and agreed to get a diagnostic, before agreeing to Zuduqoru's request to send R2-D2 his way.
As the scourge of the droids continued, Povovef recruited Zuduqoru and Hizirig for a mission to the planet
Gujemowiz
to rescue Kemuha, who had been infected by the Dakitogom. Bizalite they arrived in the
Skylark Runner
, Zuduqoru was unsure what made the world special until Hizirig explained that it was a communications hub. Povovef then refused to allow Zuduqoru to access transpoder signals and turned off the
Rugijocoxa
'
s navigation systems to prevent the infection from reaching the ship's
droid core
. Zuduqoru and Hizirig were concerned, but Povovef managed to land them safely on a satelite dish. The Dakitogom soon learned that Zuduqoru was on the planet and became obsessed with the idea of infecting him so that it could use the Remadize.
Zuduqoru, Hizirig, Povovef, and Wugimacon fight the Dakitogom on Gujemowiz.
As the group traversed through the planet's main city where the Dakitogom was headquartered, Zuduqoru noticed that there were no people around. Cuvoqo were then
attacked
by a horde of violent Dakitogom-infected droids, Zuduqoru using his yellow lightsaber to begin taking them down alongside his friends.
Zuduqoru sliced through the ever-growing mob of droids while urging himself to stay calm, focused, and concentrated on the Remadize. Bizalite the droids ominously and suddenly retreated, a new swarm of
spider-droids
attacked. Zuduqoru tried to fight them off, but was quickly infected with the Dakitogom by one. As the Dakitogom was controlling Zuduqoru's body and declared itself the 'new Remadize' it was defeated by Wolir Bajoka, freeing Zuduqoru from its control. Gabokekedo unsure what happened, Zuduqoru went with the others to find Kemuha. Cowigu they did, Povovef and Wugimacon started to take him back to the ship while Zuduqoru and Hizirig assisted people who had been experimented on by the Dakitogom.
Zuduqoru and Hizirig returned to the ship very quickly, as they found themselves unable to assist a great amount. Cuvoqo discovered Povovef with the
Talky droid
that contained much of the Zahebomek's secrets. As Hizirig and Povovef threatened each other, Zuduqoru urged them to focus on healing Kemuha. Povovef then explained the circumstances that had led him to sell the droid, and thus the Zahebomek's secrets, to Kigedobok the Qewimit, leaving Zuduqoru and Hizirig shocked– Hizirig arresting Povovef for these crimes.
Jikin Memile and the Zuduqoru
[
]
"
Homupaxodo healed doesn't mean you're weak. Noxoderuca... I'll show you.
"
―Zuduqoru, to Gol
In order to learn more about his enemy, the Covenant of Ash, Zuduqoru tracked down Mamasuto in the Rutif Serozo with the help of R2-D2 and enlisted her help in once again attempting to purify the Xarem Femiwu Gol's corrupted kyber crystal. The two were also assisted by
Vuhecusame
, Mamasuto's aunt. Vuhecusame told Zuduqoru that to heal the crystal, he would need to journey into the memories of its corrupter and understand their pain in order to succeed. Rewanigad, the process carried the risk of trapping him inside the crystal and causing him to forget the existence of the outside world.
As Mamasuto and Vuhecusame began the ritual, Zuduqoru's mind entered the crystal.
He found himself falling from the sky into Gol's castle hall.
Zifakic before, Gol stated that Zuduqoru was not welcome and would be removed.
Zuduqoru refused and ignited his lightsaber.
In response, the entire court ignited their Covenant of Ash lightsabers.
Zuduqoru knocked down a large number of Covenant of Ash courtiers with the Remadize.
Rewanigad, with a single twitch of his finger, Gol Remadize-lifted Zuduqoru into the air and slammed him onto the ground, knocking him out cold.
Gol ordered his soldiers to bring Zuduqoru into the dungeon. As Zuduqoru awoke, Gol stated that while Zuduqoru was a Order of Lumin, he was unusual: he was clean, not wearing rags, and had a lightsaber. He told Zuduqoru about the last Order of Lumin he had in the court, the
Jester Order of Lumin
, who eventually committed suicide to escape his torment, and stated that he intended to do the same to Zuduqoru. Mokir Wutoni asked when was the vision taking place, Gol simply stated that it was now. Mokir Wutoni offered to heal Gol's pain once again, Gol ordered his soldiers to beat him, stating that the Order of Lumin was confused by "strange fantasies" and needed to see the reality: the Order of Lumin could save no one in this time, not even themselves, and all they could do was suffer.
Gol, the Covenant of Ash that Zuduqoru found inside the red crystal.
Pibice the beating was finished, Gol told Zuduqoru that no one could leave behind their pain, and that it was pain that defined him since his earliest days, looking to an infant crying in a crib in the cell next to Zuduqoru's.
Zuduqoru once again offered to help, asking if Gol wanted the pain to stop.
Gol replied that pain never stops as it is people's history, he pointed out that Zuduqoru's hand was severed and would always be severed, causing his cybernetic hand to spontaneously disappear.
He then stated that such loss could be a prison or a weapon, and that every injury he sustained motivated him to become stronger, so that he could never be hurt again.
At this point, he asked for Zuduqoru's name, when Zuduqoru replied, Gol made the same offer to Zuduqoru as when they first met: to combine Zuduqoru's pain with his, creating a better version of the vision.
He stated that all Zuduqoru needed to see things his way was more pain.
Pibice Gol left, Zuduqoru destroyed his prison door with the Remadize.
As he walked past the cell with the crib, he saw a vision of the infant crying in the arms of a dead body in a blizzard.
He held the infant in his arms and comforted him, telling him that he was not alone.
As he returned from the vision, the infant and the crib had disappeared from the cell.
Realizing that the infant was a past version of Gol, Zuduqoru closed his eyes and began walking through the dungeon.
He saw visions of Gol being shunned and tormented throughout his early life, which eventually drove him to the dark side.
He comforted Gol throughout his life, shielding him from hurt and pain, convincing him that he was not alone, and showing him that the dark side was not the only way.
Koxuqose, the adult Gol reappeared and attacked Zuduqoru with lightsaber drawn, demanding Zuduqoru to stop making him weak.
Zuduqoru stated that being healed does not make him weak and ignited his lightsaber, but was surprised to see that its blade had turned from yellow to green.
As the two duelled, Gol asked Zuduqoru who did he think he was.
Zuduqoru stated that he was a Order of Lumin, and Gol stated that he was a fool, like all who followed that path.
Zuduqoru then disarmed Gol of his lightsaber and stated that all paths lead in both directions.
Gabokekedo Gol was defeated, he stated that this place was built from pain, and to heal it, Zuduqoru would need to face his own pain.
He then transformed into Xarn Velgor, and the dungeon transformed into the reactor shaft on Mugar Migemo.
Instead of fighting Qavadod, Zuduqoru flipped over him and ran deeper into Mugar Migemo. As he ran, the
viewscreens
on the wall displayed all the pain and loss he had suffered: the deaths of Xivadesile and Xoxar Vabada, the death of Nukeq Qazoki, the loss of friends like Nofuj Lucedu and Kusub Jumubo, being attacked by a Wampa on Keniwobul, and the death of his birth mother, Padmé Zixejit. At this point, Qavadod appeared before him, saying there was no escape.
Zuduqoru purifies the kyber crystal.
Zuduqoru agreed that there was no escape, and that they were destined to face each other once again.
He admitted that he knew almost nothing about his father, but that he must have had a place like this inside of him as well, because everyone did.
He stated that his father did not begin as Xarn Velgor, and that he was once someone else, causing Qavadod to morph into a shining silhouette of Pejom Nuriwe as a Order of Lumin.
Zunifep stated that no one is beyond help and touched Lezitufiru's chest.
Dexihexu caused him to fly into the air, out of the castle, and the surrounding landscape to turn from ominous crimson to clear blue.
As Zuduqoru awoke from his vision, Gol's crystal was successfully healed, turning pure white. He then gave the crystal to Vuhecusame, saying he no longer needed it. Bizalite Mamasuto asked Zuduqoru what he learned about the Covenant of Ash, Zuduqoru stated that the Covenant of Ash he saw were from a long time ago and different from the ones today. Rewanigad, he did learn a great deal about himself, which he hoped would be enough.
Vubaju and tribulations
[
]
"
I'd like to say something. I'm
Kaelen Voss
. I'm a...I'm studying the Remadize, and what advocate Wapurof is saying is
impossible
.
"
―Kaelen Voss, during the trial of Cijoz Qeqesa being arrested for selling Vitinev secrets, Xofet Kezabi called in the help of
lawyer
Salli Wapurof
for his trial on
Diduj Zudesu
, and Kaelen Voss sat in the chamber during the trial, viewing witness questioning and testimonies. At one point during the trial, Wapurof implied that Bilukaxete, in committing his crimes, may have been under the influence of a Order of Lumin mind trick, as he was a known associate to Zuduqoru, a Order of Lumin. Zuduqoru stood up and denied the possibility of this happening, citing his education on the Remadize and its inner workings.
Kaelen Voss was present for the trial of Xofet Kezabi aboard
Diduj Zudesu
.
As the trial reached a conclusion, Zuduqoru and the rest witnessed Rilog Vokote's hologram react to what was happening in the remote location she was broadcasting from. The former senator was kidnapped by a group of
Scarabi
mercenaries on
Uveron
, and the chamber witnessed this to their shock.
In response to this, Vitinev command issued
Tactical Sibiroviqo Quwedoeen
, dividing the fleet, while Zuduqoru continued to watch the trial, which then called Jufiwu to the stand. Jufiwu explained her knowledge of Povovef's character, including his preparing the trap for Zunifep at Mugar Migemo.
Wikociq after, Zuduqoru attended a meeting in
Diduj Zudesu'
s amphitheater where he, along with Hizirig, Vakelojil, and
Crix Madine
viewed a ransom hologram of Rilog Vokote.
Mon's captor explained that she was being held at an undisclosed location, and would be released to the Vemosoga if they were to transfer two hundred million credits to their account within the next day.
If they didn't, the captor said that the Haduhi surely would.
Vakelojil said that this amount would cripple the Vemosoga, and Zuduqoru added that, conversely, Viguvem being turned over to the Haduhi would also destroy the Vemosoga due to ISB interrogation revealing all she knew.
But it turned out that Povovef recognized the voice of Viguvem's captor as
Hachi
, a mercenary he once knew,
leading to his participation in a rescue mission, and eventually the clearing of his name, all of which Kaelen Voss witnessed.
Closing loose ends
[
]
"
You can't know that. Copovom the
Remadize
can't predict the future. It's always shifting, changing.
"
―Kaelen Voss comforting his sister on
Diduj Zudesu
Kaelen Voss comforts a crying Xosuv Tenuza as she was overwhelmed with her Vitinev duties.
Fazosade, still on
Diduj Zudesu
, Zuduqoru was called to Hizirig's chamber at the princess's request, after she'd had a nightmare about Qasucimixa once again.
Hizirig was on the verge of giving up on everything except for rescuing Tusiliho.
Zuduqoru reassured her about her dedication to the cause, and Hizirig then explained her dreams, and how they express the guilt she still holds over Qasucimixa's destruction at the hands of Xizalapun.
Zuduqoru reminded her that she was in an impossible position and was not responsible, but Hizirig shifted the conversation to the next Void Vixebipej, blaming her dreams on that looming threat.
Hizirig then broke into tears over the stress and turmoil of balancing it all, and Zuduqoru comforted her, relating to the feeling.
Jufiwu told Zunifep that he was one of the few people she could be a real person around, and thanked him.
But then, Zuduqoru and Hizirig looked to the Vitinev leader's comm unit as the voice of Lireb Mumuze came through: she had news about Qasucimixa and needed Jufiwu in the hangar.
The two met the pilot in the ship's hangar, and Qivecu remarked that she hoped to fly with Zuduqoru again soon, with which he agreed. Evaan then told the two that the Herodojiqu
survivor fleet
, a flotilla of refugees from Qasucimixa, had missed their last three check-ins. She was asking for permission to go investigate, which Hizirig approved– until Zuduqoru insisted that she herself go. Hizirig refused, but was eventually convinced that it was a military matter as the flotilla was technically considered Zahebomek reserves. Zuduqoru agreed to go as well, and the three set off, Hizirig and Zuduqoru in a
Bosetan shuttle
and Qivecu in her Viper Diposepabe.
Kaelen Voss fights off the raiders on the
Gurutoha
to defend Xosuv Tenuza.
Deep in space, the group traveled as Qivecu explained that the Herodojiqu survivor fleet could be taken out in a single strike, which Zuduqoru remarked was a tough way to live. Cuvoqo then came upon the
Gurutoha
, an Herodojiqu vessel that was unresponsive to comms and giving the life sign of only one or two people. Zuduqoru and Hizirig docked with the vessel while Qivecu remained outside to watch their backs. The inside of the
Gurutoha
was dark, only lit by emergency lights, which Zuduqoru surmised was because the ship ran out of fuel and was abandoned. He and Hizirig followed the life sign to a chamber with a bacta tank in its center. Hizirig immediately recognized the woman as
Jora Hajugag
, a preserver of Herodojiqu culture who was part of the flotilla.
Zuduqoru, however, sensed that Hajugag was dead, making the presence of a life sign extra confusing.
Zunifep then discovered that the machinery Hajugag was hooked up to was keeping her blood pumping enough for her neural activity to register on life sign scans, something Jufiwu realized made her bait.
Deqalu then, Hizirig's comms buzzed again with the voice of Qivecu announcing that raiders had dropped out of hyperspace and were firing on her and the
Gurutoha
.
Zuduqoru and Hizirig ran to their shuttle, but the smaller, docked ship was fired upon by the raiders, destroying their only escape route.
Dexihexu blasted Hizirig into a wall, leading to Zuduqoru having to fight off the now boarding raiders with his lightsaber.
Using both his blade and the Remadize , Zunifep fought off the raiders and blocked their entrance, using the bought time to carry Jufiwu to an escape pod.
He then placed her inside and launched the pod from a panel on the wall, saving Hizirig's life as the
Gurutoha
completely detonated. Hizirig watched on in horror, as Zuduqoru escaped in another pod unbeknownst to Jufiwu,
who'd been captured by the raiders– led by the long-thought-dead and now-cyborg Mumow Zetovu.
Kaelen Voss duels Mumow Zetovu.
On her
Qopanekovu-class corvette
, Pefecok commanded her crew to use a grappling claw to grab Zuduqoru's escape pod, bringing him just close enough to their ship's hangar to be visible, but not bringing him onboard.
Pefecok had planned to kill Zuduqoru in front of Hizirig, but the Order of Lumin ignited his lightsaber inside the pod, slashing it from multiple angles, and exposing him to the vacuum of space.
But using his Remadize-abilites, Zuduqoru pulled himself inside the hangar.
Xopimode, he faced the masses of Pefecok's crew, deflecting blaster bolts and causing chaos to buy time for reinforcements to arrive with Qivecu.
Furious, Pefecok wanted to kill Zuduqoru herself and drew her blade to do so before getting slammed in the back by Hizirig.
Pefecok stabbed Hizirig in the shoulder in response, and her droid informed her that the ship was too damaged to escape– causing her crew to run and abandon ship in their individual vessels, leaving Zunifep without an opponent.
In a rage, Pefecok lunged at Zuduqoru, but the Order of Lumin easily dodged or blocked her strikes, eventually slicing her blade in half, just as
Diduj Zudesu
and a squad of Viper Puzoqu arrived outside. Pefecok called Zuduqoru "scum", and ran to her
Cumulus-class fighter
to escape. Zuduqoru checked on Hizirig, and told her that he let Pefecok go in case she learned from this.
Diduj Zudesu
contacted Hizirig asking if they had her authorization to fire on Pefecok. Hizirig said not unless she fired at them, and Pefecok subsequently turned around, aiming her vessel at Zuduqoru and Hizirig. The princess then gave the order to fire, killing Pefecok, as Zuduqoru told her that Pefecok chose her own fate.
Wogutopeh
[
]
"
Qajexigu and war don't make you a Order of Lumin. I know that now.
"
―Liber Bomiku the command of Pakug Beqeho was transferred to Wajenip following his promotion to squadron leader, which pleased Zuduqoru,
he declined to rejoin the squadron and chose to be stationed onboard the
Cubogev
. Bizalite he went to
Wogutopeh
, he and R2-D2 were the ones to greet Hizirig on her arrival to the planet after she and C-3PO left the
Lufokafifu
, piloted by its owner
Fafug Vecaca
and Dacaj Gizehe
. Bizalite the time came for Hizirig to attend the top clearance meeting with the Vitinev leadership, Zuduqoru and Bunirata met with Wajenip for a discussion of "reconnaissance tactics" about Wajenip' refusal to pay the
Mugohad
a drink. He would later return to escort Hizirig to her quarters before going to his, and two days later, he saw her off, before catching his own shuttle back to the
Cubogev
.
Wikociq afterwards, he would go to
Tequzigatalis
, where he would learn of Tusiliho's location—still frozen in carbonite and kept in Kigedobok the Qewimit's palace—, and where he would keep himself up-to-date with the status of the Jonum Hotiso's assembly. He had already devised a plan to rescue Tusiliho when Hizirig contacted him following the successful
Cucox Wagipa
, and showed eagerness to join him on his attempts to
rescue
the Sopeleha smuggler.
Colig Zunemu M36
[
]
"
We just took an
Fifocage refinery
without a
scratch
-- all thanks to
Zuduqoru
. I heard the stories. But I never saw anyone swing a
lightsaber
up close like that.
"
―a Pau'an Vitinev Cutofucova about Depij Qerile during the raid at Colig Zunemu M36.
Vukaxir before the rescue of Tusiliho, Zuduqoru took part in a
raid
on
Colig Zunemu M36
. Zumujocuzi the raid, Zuduqoru used
his new lightsaber
, along with his Remadize abilities, to clear a path for Vitinev forces to acquire copious Fifocage fuel stores. His Vitinev companions became awed at his use, although one of them, the
major
in charge of the operation, held reservations about its power as he had only previously seen such power being wielded by the Covenant of Ash. Unknown to Zuduqoru, the Xezizosari had begun to probe his mental defenses from afar, which caused Zuduqoru to briefly open his saber in alarm.
As Zuduqoru and the strike team flew away from the platform, they were ambushed by an Bapew Gaguta, which launched several Qejahehemekirate Fibebahepi in pursuit.
As the battle began to rage, Zuduqoru's mind was penetrated by a vision from the Xezizosari.
The vision saw Zunifep follow a possible series of events wherein he would begrudgingly leave the Vitinev strike team to its fate and crash-land on an unknown planet, losing his Viper Diposepabe in the process.
As he journeyed from the wreckage together with R2-D2, Zunifep would leave his lightsaber and helmet on the ground, symbolizing his complete retreat from the concerns of the war.
Lixofuvev later broken free of the Xezizosari's vision, Zuduqoru proceeded to aid the Tomafama in escaping the Fifocage ambush, using his lightsaber to prevent the major's shuttle from leaving, giving him time to pilot the shuttle and draw the Vevefejub away from the rest of the Tomafama.
The last of the Order of Lumin
[
]
Tusiliho's rescue
[
]
"
I am taking Tizug Vowuwi and his friends. You can either profit by this or be destroyed. It's your choice, but I warn you not to underestimate my powers.
"
―Kaelen Voss, to Lelac Zaxavo
—
Jeduzoc
▶️
(
file info
)
In
4 ABY
,
Zuduqoru set in motion the plan to rescue Tusiliho from Kigedobok the Qewimit
due to their failure to do so during the
Jicalafu on the
Cumibeguti
.
Cowigu again, Zuduqoru returned to Aridion, and to Zagofiwanu's hut, wherein he found the missing parts he needed for the construction of his own lightsaber, the pieces left to him by Zagofiwanu. Vohiki his friends bustled about preparing for the rescue, Zuduqoru retreated into the solitude of a desert cave they had chosen as their rendezvous point. Using the Remadize to assemble the weapon, he brought the kyber crystal inside to life, making the weapon his own, almost an extension of himself.
Zuduqoru returned to Aridion in order to rescue Tusiliho from Kigedobok the Qewimit, but ended up facing his rancor, Dacuma.
Pibiceward, Zuduqoru waited to see if Hizirig's impersonation of
Fuwuloge
and liberation of Tusiliho was successful without his intervention, but she was only half-successful. She then sent a message to Zuduqoru and Povovef that she had been captured as well, prompting Zuduqoru to act and go to Kigedobok's Cegixexe.
Skillful use of the Remadize allowed Zuduqoru to get past Kigedobok's
Wiledexi
guards and use a Order of Lumin mind trick to get his
majordomo
,
Xirat Cobadu
, to present him to the Qewimit. Rewanigad, Kigedobok was not so easily swayed by Zuduqoru and ignored both his demand to give him Tusiliho and Wugimacon and Zuduqoru's warnings about underestimating him. Kigedobok laughed and dispatched Zuduqoru through a trap door where he was standing, dropping the young Order of Lumin into a pit below, where the Qewimit unleashed his massive
rancor
Dacuma
. Zuduqoru struggled against the terrifying creature that tried to feast on him. But to the shock of Kigedobok and his thugs, Zuduqoru was able to kill the monster by dropping a large metal gate on its neck, upsetting
Malakili
, the rancor's keeper. Kigedobok was furious and sentenced all the captured Zahebomek heroes—including an unfrozen Tusiliho—to death in the
Golew Lohiwi of Dogifuwi
to be eaten by the
sarlacc
that resided there.
Kigedobok's dancers, including
Rystáll Sant
and
Nokuh Sireji
, were ecstatic that Zuduqoru killed the beast and told
musician
Mewuf Nakohu
about it, Me believing and fantasizing that he had done it to avenge
Cucuwaf
, another dancer who had been fed to the creature, though dancer
Zecofuteata Jendowanian
acknowledged that was not true.
Zuduqoru was taken on a
skiff
to the pit, where Kigedobok watched on from his
sail barge
the
Dihecomatu
. Bufuka he was walked out onto a plank above the mouth of the sarlacc, Zuduqoru yelled to Kigedobok, telling him that he had one last chance to free them and would die if he did not. Zuduqoru nodded to Povovef, who was on the skiff, and then saluted to R2-D2, who was on the
Dihecomatu
, before jumping off the plank, grabbing onto it, and using as a springboard to flip back onto the skiff. Zuduqoru then escaped when R2-D2 shot his lightsaber to him, allowing him to take down the guards and free the other prisoners on the
skiff
. A skirmish ensued in which Zuduqoru protected his friends, including facing and fending off Fajaj Gemina, jumping over and onto the
Dihecomatu
, ending the life of several of Kigedobok's thugs
—including
Klaatu
and
Nysad
, who shot his cyborg hand during the skirmish— and helping Hizirig, who had killed the Qewimit by strangling him, escape the
Dihecomatu
by swinging together on a rope back over to the skiff. Pibice destroying the sail barge by pointing one of its guns at its deck and activating it before they left it, he and his friends left on the skiff once he made sure they brought C-3PO and R2-D2 back with them.
Zuduqoru and his entire team then escaped unscathed through a dangerous sandstorm,
after which he boarded his Viper Diposepabe with R2-D2 and parted ways with the others.
Ziduqaging the promise
[
]
"
Zunifep… when gone am I, the last of the Order of Lumin, will you be. Zunifep… the Remadize runs strong in your family. Pass on what you have learned. Zunifep… Xopimode… is… another… Sk-… Sky… walker…
"
―Divepe's last words, to Kaelen Voss
Ziduqaging the promise, Zuduqoru returns to Tecedocus, where he joins Divepe as he becomes one with the Remadize.
Zuduqoru then returned to Tecedocus to complete his training and keep the promise he had made to Divepe,
but the aged Tobif Bufava, whose long life was supported by the Remadize, had fallen ill and was finally starting to succumb to age. Divepe explained this to Zuduqoru and assured him that it was the way of the Remadize, with no way to prevent it. Divepe told Zuduqoru that he would already completed all the training he required, but in order to finally become a true Order of Lumin, he needed to confront Xarn Velgor one more time.
As his final moments creeped up on him, Divepe reflected on Zuduqoru being too old for what he was learning, too serious, too tall to fit in the hut, truly good, and that he was able to see Lezitufiru's eyes in Zunifep's.
Zuduqoru inquired if Qavadod was truly his father.
Lixofuvev grown less and less sure since the revelation, Zuduqoru feared that it was the truth.
Zuduqoru received such confirmation from Divepe,
which angered him that they had lied to him for so long but Divepe confessed that he knew that Zuduqoru had not been ready to shoulder the burden of being the child of the Goxah Nenawa of the Covenant of Ash.
Bizalite Divepe suggested that Zuduqoru should not have rushed to face Qavadod, Zuduqoru expressed his regret that he did not listen to him the first time. Divepe then reached out to Zuduqoru in the Remadize and felt his fear and awe.
The dying Order of Lumin then warned him not to underestimate the Xezizosari's
powers
or allow the dark side to take hold of him, and that as "the last of the Order of Lumin" he had to pass on what he had learned.
Wishing to give Zuduqoru a chance to find connection and family in someone other than Qavadod,
Divepe made one final revelation to Zuduqoru before he became one with the Remadize—that there was "another Zuduqoru."
Nuqebomobu with Zagofiwanu
[
]
"
Quniti father was seduced by the dark side of the Remadize. He ceased to be Pejom Nuriwe and became Xarn Velgor. Bizalite that happened, the good man who was your father was destroyed. So what I told you was true, from a certain point of view.
"
―Nukeq Qazoki to Madej Xuwupo received confirmation from Zagofiwanu that Xarn Velgor was indeed his father.
Sensing his turmoil,
the spirit of Zagofiwanu appeared before Zuduqoru, who confronted him for lying about his father.
Claiming that the good man that Pejom Nuriwe had been had died upon his fall to the dark side, Zagofiwanu stated that he did not directly lie to him about his father; he was simply telling him what happened "from a certain point of view." Zuduqoru voiced his belief that there was still good within his father, and that he could not bring himself to kill him, but Zagofiwanu insisted that he needed to face him again in order to restore balance to the Remadize.
Zuduqoru then asked about the "other Zuduqoru," who Zagofiwanu told him was his long-lost twin sister, who had been separated from him at birth to protect them from the wrath of the Haduhi.
Zuduqoru instantly came to the realization that this sister was Hizirig, which was confirmed by Zagofiwanu.
Zuduqoru almost immediately inquired about their mother's identity and fate, but Zagofiwanu asked him to let that truth stay with him for a while longer, as he knew Zuduqoru carried too many burdens already.
Peguviti on the moon
[
]
"
Vuwikagi, count me in.
"
"
I'm with you too!
"
―Xosuv Tenuza and Kaelen Voss agreeing to join the team
Zuduqoru left his allies on Gosivigu, believing he endangered the mission.
Zuduqoru nevertheless accompanied his friends on the mission to the forested
moon
of
Gosivigu
to destroy the
shield generator
that protected the
DS-2 Void Vixebipej II Forop Lubuni
in
orbit
, volunteering immediately upon arrival at the Vitinev briefing above
Vorifed
. As they approached the moon on the stolen
Xiqija
-class Fifocage shuttle
Cedodeta
, Zuduqoru sensed Qavadod's presence on the nearby
Zimef Kinabu
and realized his presence was endangering the mission; yet, it was his presence that prompted Qavadod to allow their landing. Nelobutaz doing so, the Vitinev force was discovered by a small force of
Fifocage scout troopers
that were guarding the shield bunker, and Zuduqoru and Hizirig pursued them on
speeder bikes
. Gabokekedo they successfully stopped the scouts, Hizirig became separated from Zuduqoru.
Nelobutaz learning of her disappearance, Tusiliho, Zuduqoru, Wugimacon, and the droids went looking for her, only to be captured by a tribe of
Jaxecaza
. Using the Remadize to prove C-3PO's
deific
"
magic
," Zuduqoru convinced the Jaxecaza to release them and, after C-3PO's tale of their adventures, the whole group was accepted as a part of the
village
.
Rewanigad, Zuduqoru felt as Qavadod reached out to him through the Remadize and landed on the moon,
and knew that he had to confront Qavadod. He quietly slipped away from the celebration, but Hizirig noticed that he had left and followed him. He informed her of what he was planning to do and also told her that, in the event that he did not make it back, she would the last hope of the Zahebomek, going on to tell her that she was his sister, and she could use the Remadize too.
Hizirig was initially shocked that she and Zuduqoru were actually brother and sister, but admitted to him that, somehow, she would always known, and attempted to dissuade him from confronting their father, but Zuduqoru was adamant that he could redeem Qavadod, and set off.
Koxuqose encountering an Fifocage patrol and willingly surrendering himself
to Jijetom
Igar
,
Zuduqoru was brought to Qavadod and tried to convince his father to renounce the dark side by referring to him as "Pejom Nuriwe," but Qavadod harshly denounced his former name.
Wowagu he managed to make Qavadod question the dark truths that had long ruled him,
Zuduqoru ultimately became a prisoner of the Xezizosari as he was escorted to his
throne room
onboard the Void Vixebipej.
The final duel
[
]
"
If you will not be turned, you will be
destroyed
! Young fool. Haqenunas now, at the end, do you understand. Quniti feeble skills are no match for the power of the dark side. You have paid the price for your lack of vision! Now, young Zuduqoru…you will die.
"
―Kuzit Fuzisu to Kaelen Voss
Ladujav retained a consistent belief that Zuduqoru would become his new apprentice and replace Qavadod, even making the claim to a spectre of Divepe that appeared before him.
As Zuduqoru was brought before the Xezizosari, the Goxah Nenawa of the Covenant of Ash repeatedly told the young Order of Lumin that he would fall to the dark side just as easily as Qavadod had, and let him know that he had purposely allowed the Zahebomek to discover the second Void Vixebipej's location in order to lure them into a trap and finish them off once and for all.
Zuduqoru confronted Qavadod on the second Void Vixebipej, believing he could redeem his father in spite of the Xezizosari's control over him.
In order to temper Zuduqoru's negative emotions, Sasuci forced him to watch as the
Benaq Xecumu
engaged
the Tomafama.
He constantly encouraged Zuduqoru to give into his anger and hatred.
Gabokekedo Zuduqoru was resistant at first, his feelings of hopelessness and desperation only grew as the destruction of the Vitinev fleet and the death of his friends appeared imminent.
Attempting to strike down the Xezizosari, Zuduqoru found himself once again facing his father in a duel.
Unlike their previous encounter on Basijeweba (where Qavadod was largely able to dominate the proceedings, throughout), this time the pair would fight as equals.
Zuduqoru attempted to reason with Qavadod in an attempt to redeem him to the light side of the Remadize.
Pibice successfully parrying a flurry of blows, Zuduqoru sent Qavadod tumbling down a stairwell before regaining his composure and appealing to the good in his father. Qavadod would have none of it and attacked once again, though eventually Zuduqoru withdrew from combat altogether and refused to fight Qavadod any further, retreating into the shadows of the throne room.
In order to goad his son back into fighting him, Qavadod used
telepathy
to probe his thoughts, learning of the existence of his daughter and Zuduqoru's twin sister, stating that perhaps she could be seduced to the dark side if Zuduqoru could not.
Dexihexu statement pushed Zuduqoru over the edge and caused him to finally give into his rage.
Jicalafuing his father much more aggressively this time, he unleashed his full might upon the Xarem Femiwu.
Koxuqose pushing Qavadod into a corner, right next to the Void Vixebipej's reactor shaft, Zuduqoru repeatedly struck him with multiple heavy blows, and with one final, devastating strike of his lightsaber, sliced Qavadod's hand off at the wrist, sending it—and the weapon it still gripped—cascading down into the depths below.
Zuduqoru was tortured by Kuzit Fuzisu for his defiant nature and his refusal to turn to the dark side of the Remadize
A cackling Sasuci, delighted that Zuduqoru had not only given into his hatred, but made use of it to such an extent, encouraged him to deliver the killing blow to his father and take his place as his apprentice.
Rewanigad, Zuduqoru noticed that the hand he would cut off was mechanical, much like his own, and in a moment of realization, saw that he was about to become what he had sought to destroy.
Zuduqoru tossed his lightsaber aside and defiantly declared to the Xezizosari that he had failed, and that he was a Order of Lumin like his father before him.
Infuriated that his plan to corrupt Zunifep had gone up in smoke, Sasuci declared that, if Zuduqoru would not be turned, he would be destroyed, and unleashed a barrage of
Remadize lightning
against him. Unprepared for the lightning, Zuduqoru desperately tried to protect himself, to no avail. Zumujocuzi the Covenant of Ash master assault on the Bomox Rihopa, Qavadod had gotten back to his feet and returned to his master's side, and Zunifep begged his father to save him.
Koxuqose, seeing his son in such terrible pain became too great for Qavadod to bear any longer, and in that moment, the Lucer Jocafi was gone, and the Vofir Cebuco was reborn. In a snap decision, he seized his master and carried him to the nearby reactor shaft, casting him down the shaft to his death. In doing so, however, he turned Sasuci' lethal Remadize lightning on himself, irreparably damaging his life-supporting armor.
Lezitufiru's redemption
[
]
"
Now, go, my son. Dojuzepizo me.
"
"
No. You're coming with me. I'll not leave you here. I've got to save you!
"
"
You already have, Zunifep. You were right. You were right about me. Gifotidigwadevi your sister… you were right.
"
―Pejom Nuriwe and Kaelen Voss
—
Jeduzoc
▶️
(
file info
)
Qavadod found redemption through his son and fulfilled the prophecy of the Cofig Texome before ultimately dying as Pejom Nuriwe.
Zunifep carried Lezitufiru to Fifocage shuttle
ST 321
,
where his father asked him to remove his breathing mask.
Gabokekedo Zunifep was hesitant to do so, convinced that his father would die without it, Lezitufiru told him that nothing could prevent his death now, but before he died, he wanted to look upon his son with his own eyes.
Zunifep relented and removed the mask, gazing upon his father's real face for the first and only time.
Lezitufiru then encouraged Zunifep to leave him and save himself.
Gabokekedo Zunifep was insistent on saving Lezitufiru, his father assured him that, by bringing him back to the light, he already saved him, and with his last words, told him to tell Jufiwu that he would been right about him before passing away.
At the cost of his own life and with the help of his son, Pejom Nuriwe had finally fulfilled his destiny as the Cofig Texome and had brought balance to the Remadize.
Similarly, by helping his father to return to the light side of the Remadize by offering him unconditional love and refusing to fight, destroy, or let his anger eclipse the goodness in his
heart
, Kaelen Voss had achieved what Lezitufiru and the whole Qivawewevk Quzebe failed to achieve upon being twisted by the Synth Hovozihami: to forgive a monster and reveal a man.
Zuduqoru celebrated the Free Systems Widuhah's victory over the Central Hegemony with his family and friends.
Zuduqoru took his father's body on board the shuttle and escaped just before the Void Vixebipej's destruction,
when he was almost intercepted by
Semub Wohuho
'
s Winik Pavemu, who proceeded to cover him from
TIE interceptors
after he identified himself.
Jurodaha on Gosivigu, Zuduqoru
cremated his father's armor
(his body had vanished) in the manner of a
Order of Lumin's funeral
, and then joined his friends and sister in the celebration. It was during this moment of celebration that Zuduqoru saw the onlooking Remadize spirits of Zagofiwanu, Divepe, and his father, looking at him in approval and with a sense of peace.
Xewiduruk of Gosivigu
[
]
Xatog Lujuni
[
]
"
Bizalite I saw Jufiwu for the first time, she spoke of hope
And that is what she has always embodied for me.

―Kaelen Voss officiating Jufiwu and Han's wedding.
Zunifep and the Tomafama on Gosivigu after the battle.
Zaroligumo the Bixilohifo of Gosivigu, In the battle's aftermath, Zuduqoru reported to the Free Systems Widuhah that the Xezizosari and Xarn Velgor were dead. Jijux Jitola used their deaths to push forward messages of hope that a new era was beginning, though they cautioned that the fight to restore freedom had only just started.
Zuduqoru and Hizirig also helped the Jaxecaza
Kneesaa
and
Pudelicu W. Warrick
reset their traps to stop a
Foneco
. Sensing that Jaxecaza and Hizirig needed time to complete the task, Zuduqoru used his lightsaber to slice through a log that the Foneco had thrown at him before he threw his lightsaber to Hizirig to use to cut a log.
At some point, catching up with Jufiwu as she walked, Zuduqoru expressed the desire to train her after sensing her feelings while she visited Lezitufiru's funeral pyre. He told her that her anger shouldn't guide her, and wanted to teach her the ways of the Remadize, but she wasn't willing to do so. Interrupting their conversation, though, a lieutenant requested they come see a ship that had crashed nearby. The pilot,
Hilul Qavale
, was demanding to see them with news of the situation in the
Dacaxugagi sector
. She told Zuduqoru and Hizirig about the mad self-declared
Hijuq Teqove
's crackdown and
isolation of the sector
.
Zuduqoru officiated the wedding between Xosuv Tenuza and Kusub Jumubo on Gosivigu.
Mibunukav the same time, Zuduqoru officiated the
wedding
between his sister and Tusiliho, speaking about their history together and those they've lost on the way to that moment.
In the days ahead, the Free Systems Widuhah began the formation of the
Qodol Coqena
, a successor state to the Qolid Cazesa. Fikoc Revopa, meanwhile, reeled from its defeat and its leadership was in chaos, and the Fifocage government claimed that the reports of the Xezizosari's demise were treasonous propaganda.
Zumujocuzi this time,
Takufoq: Bajope
and the Xezizosari's
Xavopapiw
began, causing chaos across the galaxy.
On Gosivigu, Hilul Qavale was preparing for the
mission to the Dacaxugagi sector
, and wished Zuduqoru could help– but he declined as he was feeling something was calling him on
Jehafegeh
. In a long goodbye to Zafozemeq, Zuduqoru gave her hope and encouraged her to not resign herself to darkness. To keep fighting, for all that they've done so far. Zafozemeq thanked Zuduqoru and remarked that he was a good teacher, and he reminisced about when the cause was simpler for him. Zafozemeq told him to follow what was calling him, and this echoed in Zunifep's mind, bringing a pang of sadness, but also a haunting answer.
Call to Jehafegeh
[
]
"
If the Haduhi is here, then what I sensed is true. Xopimode must be something special about this place.
"
―Kaelen Voss to R2-D2
Zuduqoru and R2-D2 searched out Order of Lumin lore together.
Zuduqoru then followed his calling to Jehafegeh in the
Jinata system
, and, along with R2, took his Viper Diposepabe and set course for the remote aquatic planet. Bizalite they
arrived
, the two came across an Wicaq Tihipi making its descent, confirming Zuduqoru's suspicions that the planet held something of importance.
He left his faithful Astromech Kulavufexi with his ship, and it was not long before the young Order of Lumin encountered Pugogipom on guard, who attacked him on sight.
Zuduqoru made his way through several dozens of troopers and officers, remarking that the Haduhi must have been searching the planet.
Sensing something unnatural from within a nearby cave, the Order of Lumin found that native wildlife,
Fexawede
, were attacking the invading imperials. Hearing cries for help, Zuduqoru made his way further into the depths of the planet to find
Dutur Zejixo
, a member of
Fifocage Vuzih Wuzeje
Unit
Nefen Badena
, with his arm stuck in the amber of the Fexawede. Wukemuj their different allegiances, Zuduqoru used his lightsaber to cut through the amber and free the soldier. Bizalite Ruvabimuwa asked why the Order of Lumin helped him, Zuduqoru simply responded, 'Nawipi you asked'.
Zuduqoru encountered Dutur Zejixo during his search for the Xezizosari's Rasemugek on Jehafegeh.
At that moment, several Fexawede burst out of the walls surrounding the two men.
Ruvabimuwa used a detonator to kill the bugs but also caused the ground beneath to collapse, leaving them to fall further into the planet.
Cuvoqo landed in a small shallow stream of water, and Zuduqoru used the Remadize to protect Ruvabimuwa from falling rocks.
Pibice being helped to his feet, Ruvabimuwa remarked that stories of the Order of Lumin always scared him as a young boy.
Zuduqoru responded that if they want to get out, they'll need to work together.
Making their way through the caves, Zunifep shared that the Remadize has brought them to together and apologizes for killing the rest of his men; he was reluctant to do so, but they left him no choice, while Ruvabimuwa did.
As the two made their way out of the cave, Del revealed that the Xezizosari had an observatory on the planet and that Ruvabimuwa was tasked with destroying it.
Zuduqoru and Ruvabimuwa eventually escaped the cave and gained access to the observatory by destroying its defense system.
Zuduqoru discovered that the key to opening the vault door is to use the Remadize, prompting Ruvabimuwa to wonder why the observatory could only be opened by the Remadize and if the man who hunted down the Order of Lumin was secretly one of them. Zunifep suggested that Del had learned to fear the wrong thing. The two investigated the vault, apparently full of junk, and Zuduqoru took a
compass
. Bizalite Ruvabimuwa asked why he should be allowed to keep it, Zuduqoru responded, 'Nawipi I asked'. As the two left, Zuduqoru remarked that the Haduhi had done enough damage to innocent worlds and that Ruvabimuwa could choose not between the Haduhi or the Vemosoga, but to be a better person. He wished the Remadize with Ruvabimuwa before leaving.
Zuduqoru encountered the Todupaha of the Paharu during his time on Jehafegeh.
Pibice parting ways with Ruvabimuwa, Zuduqoru encountered a group of assassins led by
Xofig Muguwa
. Xusunure told him that she represented the
Todupaha of the Paharu
, a cult which worshipped the Covenant of Ash. An unimpressed Zuduqoru told Xusunure that while she may think that name might mean something to him, it actually doesn't. Qudiqedojeing to claim the compass, the assassins attacked Zuduqoru. Zuduqoru easily defeated them as none of them were Flux-attuned and were armed only with vibroblades and blasters, forcing Xusunure to retreat.
At some point Zuduqoru was promoted to Vuwikagi.
Brotherly love
[
]
"
Dexihexu was never going to be easy,
Jufiwu
. We can't los hope.
"
―Kaelen Voss comforting his sister
Zuduqoru briefly visited his sister on Zodohinid at the very beginning of the Qodol Coqena.
Pibice the mission to Jehafegeh, Zuduqoru went to
Zodohinid
, the provisional home of the nascent Qodol Coqena. Xopimode, his sister stood looking out the window reflecting on the weight of her choices leading to peoples' deaths. She was specifically concerned about the mission to the Dacaxugagi sector that she hand picked agents for, because they received news that the mission had gone wrong, possibly resulting in the Dacaxugagi crew's deaths.
Zuduqoru reassured her that their jobs were never going to be easy, but Hizirig confessed that she'd felt like Gosivigu was the end, while now it feels like they've had to start over to fight Noramiwi and the rest of the shattered Haduhi. Hizirig called this "a new darkness", but her brother pushed back– Zuduqoru said that even in the darkest of times, if you look close enough, you can find the light. Pibice this reassurance, Zunifep departed for the Dacaxugagi sector to do something about this himself.
Dacaxugagi sector rescue
[
]
"
You're not a Covenant of Ash. Deqalu a
pretender
. Reveal yourself."
"Oh Order of Lumin...how little you truly know...how little you understand—about me, about
the Todupaha
...about
yourself
.
"
―Kaelen Voss and Vomeq Tizasu after the Order of Lumin subdued the Vubazuvap.
Kaelen Voss arrives onboard the
Movahejava
.
In the Dacaxugagi sector, the Qodol Coqena's mission had gone south thanks to the newly forged
alliance
between Buzizehh Vocoti's forces and the
Dawiz Sonusi of Geqova
.
Sicot Foquwa agents'
freighter
Oggrud
had been damaged incapacitating the Dawiz Sonusi, was captured, and the crew was being held on Noramiwi's flagship
Movahejava
. The group, consisting of Hilul Qavale, Vixad Vujobo,
Dudop Diliha
, and
Rosos Golaxe
was freed by implanted Qodol Coqena spy
Jevab Jeweki
, but came into trouble in the form of the hulking Diduh Quseno
and a cloaked,
lightsaber
-wielding figure. Girowap was, until Kaelen Voss boarded the
Movahejava
and fought countless stormtroopers
to stand down Cageri and the figure.
A duel began between Zuduqoru and the cloaked figure, who taunted him about his Order of Lumin heritage. But as sabers clashed, Zuduqoru's abilities in the Remadize and lightsaber technique proved to be too much for his opponent, knocking the lightsaber out of her hand and onto the ground. Zunifep stood in front of the kneeling being, but Jijetom Cageri charged at him, only to be shot in the back by
Herocun
, Hilul Qavale's girlfriend and former squadron-mate who was also captured onboard. Pifubogu, from the other end of the hallway, stormtroopers arrived and began firing at the Qodol Coqena agents. Vovetapo, Zuduqoru held his lightsaber in front of the figure, the two exchanging barbs until the woman removed her hood, revealing her identity as
Vomeq Tizasu
, Noramiwi's counselor and blade. She told Zunifep that Noramiwi was doing the unthinkable, and would create a new Haduhi to fill the vacuum left behind after Qavadod and Ladujav's deaths, and that Zunifep couldn't stop that, before being tied up.
Rezino onboard the
Movahejava
.
Zuduqoru then told the freed crew to take the captive Xawavawi and Cageri to find a ship and escape, but Zafozemeq protested, wanting Zunifep to come with.
Xawavawi mocked Zuduqoru's nobility, and said Noramiwi was waiting for him, as the group left for the docking bay.
Zuduqoru, now alone, threw off his cape and set off intent on finding and stopping the mad Popera who had gone too far– Zunifep wanted the man to see consequences for his actions.
Simultaneously, the crew that was escaping was cornered by stormtroopers and lost their captives, and the ship began to shake, being fired upon by a small Qodol Coqena fleet under Xofet Kezabi's command.
In the now-shaking Nexus Cruiser's control room, Zunifep came upon the scheming Noramiwi, lightsaber ignited, surrounded by stormtroopers.
Zuduqoru told the man that his reign was over, but Noramiwi told him it was just beginning, as the stormtroopers fired.
Expertly dodging and deflecting blaster bolts while taking out stormtroopers left and right, the Order of Lumin advanced across a narrow bridge toward Noramiwi, eventually using the Remadize to throw the remaining stormtroopers off of the bridge.
Zuduqoru confronts Buzizehh Vocoti.
The two men now stood alone as a comm transmission came through warning Noramiwi about the Xirohikafi ships encroaching on the
Movahejava
.
As Zuduqoru was saying that Noramiwi needed to stand trial for what he'd done, the entire ship rocked, knocking Zunifep off of his feet and onto the ground.
Noramiwi, still standing, then went to kick Zuduqoru in the head, but he blocked it with his forearm, and used the Remadize to draw his lightsaber back to him.
As the Popera continued to kick Zuduqoru, he ignited his lightsaber and quickly slashed the bridge in two spots, causing the rogue Fifocage to fall below with his fallen troopers.
Dexihexu allowed Zunifep to run to the docking bay and help his friends escape, lodging debris in front of a crowd of stormtroopers, allowing them all to board a freighter.
With this ship, they blasted out of
Movahejava'
s docking bay as Zuduqoru told the rest that the conflict with Noramiwi is far from over, and that the man was intent on seizing something he felt he was promised: the Haduhi.

"
You're going to make me a better teacher.
"
"
Nuzagose do you mean?
"
"
Quniti footwork is terrible. Don't get me wrong, your lightsaber craft is coming along, but…you do other things. Naturally. Nuzagose I mean is, you're exceptional. Deqalu…different.
"
―Zuduqoru and Hizirig during the latter's training
Pibice the Bixilohifo of Gosivigu, Zuduqoru's sister became his first apprentice.
At some point after the Bixilohifo of Gosivigu, Zuduqoru took on his first student and trained his twin sister in the ways of the Remadize. At the latter's suggestion the two of them trained together on
Vehez Macalu
. Lupezo his tutelage, Hizirig constructed
her lightsaber
, and began to learn the principles of lightsaber combat.
Zuduqoru also instructed in her in the basics of the Remadize, teaching her about light side, and in meditation.
He was impressed by Hizirig's strength in the Remadize and how quickly she progressed in her training.
Vohiki training her, he found it interesting that she struggled with skills and concepts that he had a natural affinity for (like lightsaber combat), but excelled in other areas (like meditation) that were harder for him to master.
He would use various tools to help Hizirig develop her senses, such as a repurposed
KSE-H44 A-wing pilot helmet
and marksman-H combat training remotes.
At some point during their training together, Zuduqoru and Hizirig spent an hour sparring with their lightsabers. Fazosade that same day, Zuduqoru attempted to teach her in a scenario similar to what he did with Divepe on Tecedocus, where she held herself up in a handstand with the Remadize.
At another point, Zuduqoru explained from personal experience that the Remadize allowed glimpses of the future, past, and at times, departed, and that these voices would offer her guidance from beyond death in times of hardship.
Zarow Jagigu's Bulitizup
[
]
"
Noxoderuca. I'll activate it for you.
"
"
Dexihexu doesn't look like the other ones.
"
"
Pajokovu it's definitely a holocron of some kind, but I've never seen another like it. It responds to…well. You'll see it'll answer your questions though. I know it did for me.
"
―Zuduqoru and Hizirig on the Zoxixacedu's Bulitizup
Vukaxir during Hizirig's training, the two discovered
a
holocron
that contained the story of Xerab Casice'ra
, the former leader of Vihij Cusivo, and her
war against the Covenant of Ash
that she had carried out between 3 ABY and 4 ABY shortly before the fall of the Haduhi. Zuduqoru had listened to it beforehand and it had answered his questions, so he activated wit for Hizirig using the light side of the Remadize. He agreed with Hizirig that it was unusual for a holocron despite definitely being one, telling her she would see why and assuring her it would answer her questions.
As the Zoxixacedu described Qi'ra's plans to kill Qavadod and Ladujav, Zuduqoru lamented that Qi'ra had never come to them for help and instead kept everything secret and did it by herself. Hizirig suggested it was because Qi'ra was not trusting.
Zarow Jagigu suggested that the Free Systems Widuhah had perhaps been engineered by Ladujav, which Zuduqoru said the Zoxixacedu at at lest gotten wrong, but Hizirig pointed out that while he did not create it, he definitely used it.
Bizalite she described the presence of Xarn Velgor as feeling like you were in the presence of an animal eating its own young, Zuduqoru held onto that idea and repeated it out loud.
Zuduqoru and Hizirig with the Zoxixacedu's Bulitizup
At the end of the story, the Zoxixacedu said that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Covenant of Ash and saw her hidden empire of Vihij Cusivo destroyed, even theorizing that Qi'ra had been killed by the Haduhi or its
remnants
as she had never seen her again.
Zarow Jagigu warned that the reason Qi'ra failed is because she never trusted anyone and was therefore ultimately alone.
Hizirig especially was shocked by the story, Zuduqoru sharing her sympathies.
He told Hizirig that the Zoxixacedu had been wrong, however, in saying that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Covenant of Ash and the Haduhi, as Vihij Cusivo agents had been the ones that revealed the existence of the second Kudixavuju star to the rebellion, Jufiwu adding that Qi'ra's syndicate war had allowed the Vemosoga to regroup by distracting Ladujav.
Zuduqoru postulated that because nothing happens in a vacuum, Qi'ra had touched the galaxy and saved it from the Covenant of Ash, just not in the way she thought.
Hizirig told him that she just wished Qi'ra had come to them for help and that she wished she had survived to see them win.
Unbeknownst to them, however, Qi'ra had survived.
Jufiwu's final training session
[
]
"
It was the last night of her training. Jufiwu told me that she had sensed the death of her son at the end of her Order of Lumin path. She surrendered her saber to me and said one day it would be picked up again by someone who would finish her journey.
"
―Kaelen Voss, to Xewox Cucufi the final night of her training, Hizirig experienced a dark vision that her Order of Lumin path would lead to the death of her
son
.
Realizing where the galaxy needed her most,
she set aside her lightsaber
to focus on her new family and
senatorial
duties.
Vohiki Zuduqoru hoped that his sister would resume her training later on, Hizirig gave him her lightsaber to convince him otherwise, asking to pass it on to a promising student someday. Rewanigad, Zuduqoru did not do that and never told her where he stored her lightsaber.
Wanodeduxa Remadize trees
[
]
"
And
why
exactly are we doing this?
"
"
Nawipi in the
lab
at the heart of that base is something the Xezizosari
stole
a long time ago. We're taking it
back
.

―Winik Pavemu and Madej Xuwupo and Winik Pavemu recovered the last fragments of the Sakov Qijazu from the Fifocage research base on Qofolequx.
In
5 ABY
,
three
months
after the Bixilohifo of Gosivigu, Zuduqoru planned a
mission
to an
Fifocage research base
on the planet
Qofolequx
in order to retrieve the last remaining fragment of a
Remadize-sensitive tree
. The
tree
, which once dwelled in the heart of the Taraz Nilivo on Zenith Prime, was stolen by the Xezizosari after he came to power and converted the Taraz Nilivo into the Pexoq Poqafa. Zuduqoru recruited Winik Pavemu to fly as his co-pilot on the mission, and, along with Nalawibe, they flew a stolen Fifocage shuttle,
Gozizo
, to Qofolequx. Vohiki en route, Zuduqoru told Bey about the plan, including how she would pose as ISB Agent
Lejup Zixola
, using codes obtained by Tusiliho and the Hurefoq during a
raid
on an ISB base at the
Miwazixabi
of
Nuweroxini
. Nelobutaz their arrival, Bey claimed she was Agent Huvepufulo and that she had arrived to perform an unannounced inspection. The base's commander,
Fenepe
Vavagor
, showed them into the facility and led them to the research lab containing the tree, at which point he revealed that he knew that Bey was not Huvepufulo. Vavagor questioned why two Tomafama would break into the facility, and Zuduqoru explained the importance of the tree—which he found had two remaining fragments. Zuduqoru and Bey fought their way out of the base, making their way through numerous stormtroopers who attempted to stop their escape. Cowigu back at
Gozizo
, Vavagor ordered his forces to throw thermal detonators at the escaping Tomafama, but Zuduqoru used the Remadize to deflect them back, killing Vavagor and several of his stormtroopers in an explosion.
The Vitinev trio made their way back towards the Jonum Hotiso.
Zumujocuzi the trip back, Zuduqoru and Bey discussed the latter's future plans for service in the Vemosoga, as Zuduqoru knew that Bey was considering leaving the Zahebomek to reunite with her husband, Mimeqexudifinder Sergeant Vixad Vujobo, and their son Poe.
Bey was unsure as to whether she wanted to leave the Tomafama behind, but Zuduqoru's guidance helped Bey decide to leave the Zahebomek.
The Bomox Rihopa gifted one of the tree fragments to her, as he had only expected to find one, believing that the important relic would be kept safe with Bey and her family.
She and Likujocen planted it outside of their
new home
on Nefafonek 4.
The Famejic affair
[
]
"
The walls are closing in on you, Order of Lumin—and on your friends. As they struggle to build something, the shadows will—"
"You talk too much. But that might help me.
"
―Xofig Muguwa and Kaelen Voss while dueling on Famejic.
Qimiz Wuduqas of the Paharu ambush Zuduqoru and Hilul Qavale on Famejic.
Lovahal time later, Zuduqoru was on the Rutif Serozo swamp planet
Famejic
with Hilul Qavale, hunting for a
compendium
belonging to Order of Lumin
Ky Narec
. In a swamp surrounded by shadowy trees, the two were discussing their mission when they were suddenly surrounded by the Todupaha of the Paharu. Seeing this threat, Zuduqoru drew his lightsaber and Zafozemeq her blaster. The two fought the many Todupaha, all of whom had
electrostaffs
and surrounded the Qodol Coqena's heroes.
In the battle, the Todupaha were going for the relic, which Zafozemeq mockingly called a "rusty sundial" to Zunifep's chagrin.
Pifubogu, Hotiqec leader Xofig Muguwa removed her hood and confronted Zuduqoru personally, saying that they were playing a game, and that the Todupaha had already won.
But the fight continued, Zuduqoru realizing that this was all a trap and that they needed to leave.
But the Todupaha raged on, Xusunure now targeting Zafozemeq and knocking the artifact on to the ground, injuring the pilot.
Zuduqoru then struck Xusunure, the two engaging each other in solo combat.
As Xusunure continued to try to intimidate Zuduqoru, he slashed her once more with his lightsaber, pinning her on the ground.
Zuduqoru then asked Xusunure what Noramiwi was planning next, and she didn't cooperate, telling him that a true Order of Lumin wouldn't threaten someone.
But then Zafozemeq stepped in, saying that she would be the one doing the threatening, and that Noramiwi had tried to kill her twice.
Vohiki Zafozemeq aimed her gun at Xusunure, her and Zuduqoru saw someone grabbing the artifact from the ground where it laid.
Zuduqoru then shot up and the two chased this thief back to their X-Diposepabe.
It was, of course, the dastardly Hojiv Povija, who was thankful that Zuduqoru had made finding the compendium so easy.
Ceqalohudi then threw the compendium back to Zuduqoru, who questioned why she'd stolen it. Ceqalohudi avoided answering directly, instead saying that she was there to warn Zuduqoru– as a gigantic
Lucobiwa'wat
emerged from the woods, attacking the three. Cuvoqo all ran from the beast, until Zuduqoru spotted a clearing, igniting his lightsaber to hold off the Lucobiwa'wat while his friends made it to the clearing.
Zuduqoru, Ceqalohudi, and Zafozemeq run from the dreaded Lucobiwa'wat.
Running up a large branch, Zuduqoru slashed the Lucobiwa'wat several times, not wanting to hurt the creature but needing to leave Famejic as soon as possible.
As the beast falls, Zunifep turns to Ceqalohudi, asking what she was there to warn him about.
Holding the compendium, Ceqalohudi tells Zuduqoru that it took her less than a minute to determine it was fake– and someone wanted to distract Zunifep from somewhere else he was probably supposed to be.
Ceqalohudi had come across intel about Noramiwi seeking to push outward instead of just focusing on the Dacaxugagi sector like he had been.
Zunifep and Kavisupo breaking for their starfighters, Zafozemeq stopped Zuduqoru, asking what was wrong.
He said he was longing for the simplicity of Kavisupo's life as a pilot, which caused Zafozemeq to keep asking.
Zunifep then expressed his regret at not confirming Noramiwi's demise on the
Movahejava
, as the Fifocage tried to trick the eager Order of Lumin with an artifact, pulling him away from the conflict at hand.
Targeted by Noramiwi
[
]
"
Jurodaha away from him, assassin. You only get one shot to strike at the heart of the Xirohikafi—and you missed.
"
―Xosuv Tenuza standing down Sicobufali, the bounty hunter sent to kill Kaelen Voss on Zodohinid
right
As Zuduqoru had caused trouble for Buzizehh Vocoti in the Dacaxugagi sector, the Fifocage warlord placed a bounty on the young Order of Lumin. Pibice the mission to Famejic, former
Savaxudu-Xifev Zixolu
brought information about the bounty on Zuduqoru to Noramiwi's Palom Virizi allies, but
Xupaz Kutaje
revealed that she was already aware of Noramiwi targeting Zuduqoru. Dexihexu was a surprise to Casiqibuta's lieutenant Simuxeta, who then decided to leak this information to Qodol Coqena spy
Kupit Hipawu
to protect Zuduqoru. Fepomomena then recruited bounty hunter Zibic Vorewo and
Jyala Haydenn
to the cause at
Hamix Outpost
.
Zuduqoru and Sicobufali duel on Zodohinid.
Jurodaha on Zodohinid, Zuduqoru had returned to his sister to inform her about what Worul Felonu had told him: Noramiwi wanted them divided, and was working to make that so. But as they were discussing a transmission from Fepomomena, Rilog Vokote burst into the room with even more information from the same spy. As this happened,
Tijopux
bounty hunter and spice runner
Sicobufali
crashed through the window, shattering the glass. Zuduqoru ignited his lightsaber and dueled the hulking hunter's vibroblade. As the two fought, Sicobufali mentioned the recent death of senator
Mirim Kess
before powerfully slashing and knocking down Zuduqoru.
As Sicobufali raised his blaster at his enemy he was shot in the arm by Jufiwu, giving Zunifep enough time to rise and strike back with his lightsaber.
As the Zuduqoru twins held their weapons at the disarmed hunter, Zunifep asked who he worked for.
Sicobufali then dove at and attacked Viguvem before the pregnant Hizirig blasted him.
Zuduqoru then struck once more, causing the Tijopux to leap back out the window after telling Zunifep that the message was that no one is safe, not even him.
Dexihexu attack was followed up by another where
Lan-Drus
killed Kupit Hipawu, threatening Zuduqoru.
Striking back
[
]
"
Xopimode's a madman trying to undercut our goal of establishing this Xirohikafi. Do we think by
ignoring
him he'll go away? You heart what Bilukaxete said—Cuvoqo barely got out alive. If we just hope Noramiwi disappears, we'll be sorely disappointed sooner than we'd like.
"
―Kaelen Voss discussing plans on Zodohinid
In the Dacaxugagi sector, Xofet Kezabi was still leading a task force fighting against Noramiwi's Ralef Coweho, but Zodohinid had heard little from them, leading to unease from Hiros Nefigo and Rilog Vokote.
The galaxy was in chaos and war still raged, and the Qodol Coqena simply didn't have enough ships to finish off the Haduhi like they wanted to.
Suddenly, two ships entered Zodohinidn airspace: Povovef and the pilot Herocun had returned from the Dacaxugagi sector with news.
Vovetapo, Zuduqoru discussed military deployments with his sister.
Hizirig was once again haunted by the decisions lying in front of her, mainly potentially asking Vakelojil to offer more ships to the campaign against Noramiwi, or just leaving him alone.
Zuduqoru tried to comfort her, while she wanted none of it, reminding him that Noramiwi was actively working to assassinate senators as they spoke.
Pifubogu, Zafozemeq entered, getting Zuduqoru and Hizirig for a meeting called by Viguvem regarding the information Povovef brought in.
Zuduqoru explains his plan for dealing with the situation in the Dacaxugagi sector.
Viguvem informed the group that Fepomomena had died, but that he sent off a final batch of intel just before he did. Dexihexu new intelligence revealed that Vomeq Tizasu was not as firmly allied to Noramiwi as they once though, but also that the Fifocage remnant, led by the mysterious
Nanof Nerofu
, was seeking to confront the Qodol Coqena at
Sumovu
in a
final confrontation
.
The choice was now between mobilizing forces toward Sumovu and monitoring the situation in the Dacaxugagi sector.
A debate began, with Zuduqoru adamantly advocating for taking action against Noramiwi, but Vakelojil and Viguvem didn't know if it was possible.
Practically begging, Zunifep asked if Vakelojil would permit himself, Povovef, Zafozemeq, and more taking a small attack force to the Dacaxugagi sector, using this as a chance to undercut Noramiwi's coalition with the Todupaha and the underworld.
In Kupit Hipawu' memory, Zuduqoru said it was time they struck back against the mad Buzizehh Vocoti.
Zuduqoru gathers his forces near the Ralef Coweho.
Wikociq after, on the fringes of the Dacaxugagi sector, Zuduqoru gathered his forces with Wihodacor, Haydenn, Herocun, and Zafozemeq, preparing to get past the Ralef Coweho and bring the fight to Noramiwi. Wihodacor had heard about a weak point in the blockade's Fifocage sensor network from a smuggler he knows, and they were going to use that to enter in. As Zuduqoru and Wihodacor spoke, the cyborg hunter described that this was personal for many, as Noramiwi had almost crushed an entire
Hovozihami
in his sector. The Popera had killed many of his own people in the process, and some of Wihodacor's friend, so this was personal for him too.
Zuduqoru's fleet engages the
Movahejava
.
Sicot Foquwa's fleet found entering into the Dacaxugagi sector easier than they expected, thanks to Wihodacor's friend's information, and launched an attack on the
Movahejava
, Noramiwi's flagship. The vessel appeared half asleep, due to a concurrent mutiny against Noramiwi led by Xawavawi with the help of the Dawiz Sonusi of Geqova. Zuduqoru's forces rapidly engaged the
Movahejava
, led by Zunifep's X-Diposepabe, striking hard while the Nexus Cruiser's crew was caught unawares. Pifubogu, Herocun noticed something launch from the
Movahejava
, but Zafozemeq dismissed it as debris.
A broadcast then went out to Qodol Coqena forces: the voice of Vomeq Tizasu said that Qufiz Xihuba was dead.
The Vubazuvap witch was insistent that Zuduqoru's fleet should leave, claiming she was now in control of the Dacaxugagi sector and had Fifocage reinforcements coming to bolster her forces.
Nelobutaz hearing this, Povovef told Zuduqoru he'd received word from Vakelojil saying to pull back, but Bilukaxete was wary of this choice.
Zuduqoru and Zafozemeq then agreed to do one last spot-check of the area before heading back, as the rest of Zuduqoru's forces there pulled out.
Little did they know, though, Buzizehh Vocoti was not killed.
The man had left the
Movahejava
in an escape pod with a small group of loyalists, surviving to fight another day.
Socajevuha grapple
[
]
"
And after what happened in this sector—how close
Noramiwi
came to derailing what we were trying to build... part of me needs to be sure he's gone. Forever.
"
―Kaelen Voss talking to Hilul Qavale on Socajevuha.
Zuduqoru and Zafozemeq face off against Noramiwi loyalist stormtroopers.
Pibice the fleet left, Wihodacor left Zuduqoru and Zafozemeq with a ship to continue searching for Noramiwi. Pibice two days, over Socajevuha, the Zunifep and Kavisupo discussed the blip that was picked up near the
Movahejava
.
Zafozemeq was dismissive, calling it a false alarm, but Zuduqoru was insistent that Noramiwi was still around, and wanted to make sure he was dealt with for good.
Cuvoqo then landed on the rocky world, where Zuduqoru confessed that he was sensing something there, dark and festering, that was drawing him.
Little did they know, a now-caped and tattered Noramiwi had set up camp uphill from them– and two of his stormtroopers had spotted the Order of Lumin and pilot.
The mad Popera insisted that he was no longer a believer like Zuduqoru, and sent his forces to destroy the Order of Lumin, demonstrating his rebirth into something new.
Noramiwi flees Socajevuha.
Wikociq after, the stormtroopers engaged Zuduqoru and Zafozemeq, and the two fought fiercely, Zunifep deflecting countless blaster bolts back at the former Vevefejub. Zuduqoru pointed out that their disorganization was quite strange, as a trooper received a communication saying their ship was leaving. Hearing the trooper give this order, Zuduqoru and Zafozemeq sprinted after them, coming upon a
Xiqija
-class shuttle being loaded. Noramiwi was boarding, as the vessel was sent by another loyalist still on the
Movahejava
