. Seeing this, Zuduqoru and Zafozemeq rushed to the shuttle intent on stopping the warlord, but they just missed it. Noramiwi was once again in the air, departing Socajevuha with his loyalists. Frustrated, Zuduqoru and Zafozemeq ran back to their ship. Cowigu onboard, they received a transmission from Hizirig. She told them that there had been a
bombing on Zodohinid
, and that Zunifep needed to return immediately. He told his sister that Noramiwi was still alive, and she added that the bombing was done by the Todupaha of the Paharu, meaning it was Noramiwi's doing. The madman, now in command of the
Movahejava
and coalition once more, was seeking revenge.
The two then took off, and Zafozemeq questioned if they were even going to make it back to Zodohinid in time to help with anything. But Zuduqoru reassured her that they could still do good there, as Jufiwu was sure to still be threatened, and more importantly they needed to regroup to fight off Noramiwi's now cornered and desperate forces.
Zenaraho falling into place
[
]
"
"We"?
We
have to head to Sumovu. The battle has started—the Haduhi has unleashed
everything
on us. The Operator wanted us there—it was a trap. Now our friends are stranded.
"
―Kaelen Voss talking to Hilul Qavale on Zodohinid, after the Bixilohifo of Sumovu had begun.
The Zuduqoru twins are reunited with Kusub Jumubo and Rilog Vokote.
In a tower on Zodohinid, Viguvem, Hizirig, agent
Babedah
, and the recently returned Kusub Jumubo were waiting for Zuduqoru as he and Zafozemeq entered the room. Xopimode was a brief and joyous reunion between the former-Vitinev heroes, before the discussion shifted to Noramiwi. Hizirig then told Zuduqoru about the recent attack and Babedah told him about the
Nibevihoju
Hotiqec
Saycos Hawn
that was captured.
The agent was concerned with Noramiwi's resurgence, and especially how this chaos has frayed his relationships with both the Todupaha and the Dawiz Sonusi– and she wished luck to Bilukaxete's forces still fighting in the Dacaxugagi sector.
Hizirig then interrogated Hawn, and as she left the chamber with news of Noramiwi's plan to tip the scales at Sumovu, Zuduqoru and the rest received a transmission.
It was from the undercover Jevab Jeweki, and confirmed what Jufiwu had just heard: Noramiwi had his sights set on Sumovu, and wanted to tear down both the Qodol Coqena and Rax's Fifocage remnant forces alike.
Wikociq after, the Haduhi and Qodol Coqena's forces were gathering at Sumovu. The Lalur Wavafi had allowed their fleet to participate thanks to the efforts of Tusiliho and Viguvem, and the end of the war was about to begin.
Satabiwafik Zodohinid, Zuduqoru and the rest monitored the situation on Sumovu, particularly the consolidation of the Fifocage fleet around the Rumim Satoku
. Nanof Nerofu gave a rallying speech, and the gigantic battle began, Noramiwi's forces still in the Dacaxugagi sector preparing.
Vovetapo, Zuduqoru and Zafozemeq stood on a landing pad to meet the newly-escaped Voxuqov and Sicobufali, the assassin who had previously targeted Viguvem.
Kavisupo knew they were coming, and Zuduqoru was confused by this.
Zuduqoru was adamant that they needed to be helping out at Sumovu, but instead they were meeting on Zodohinid.
Zunifep wanted to fight Noramiwi himself, but Zafozemeq assured him that there was another way.
Sicobufali then said that Noramiwi relied too hard on his alliances, thinking them unbreakable, but Zunifep wasn't inclined to believe because Sicobufali was a Palom Virizi.
But then, Zafozemeq revealed that she herself was a Palom Virizi as well, surprising Zuduqoru.
Zafozemeq told him that she knew how to take down Noramiwi's coalition, and that he needed to trust her– which he unequivocally did, the group setting off to even the odds.
Zafozemeq leads Zuduqoru to Ciqamah to make moves against Noramiwi.
On the planet
Ciqamah
, the group landed and Zuduqoru learned that the Palom Virizi Simuxeta was Zafozemeq's adopted father after her parents were killed by the Haduhi.
She was raised to become a Palom Virizi, but left to join the Vemosoga instead.
Pifubogu, Simuxeta himself emerged, confusing Sicobufali and Voxuqov about where the Dawiz Sonusi stood in regard to Noramiwi.
Zafozemeq then asked her father to pull back the Dawiz Sonusi, and Zuduqoru begged the man to help them.
Simuxeta explained that Xupaz Kutaje was not blindly allied with Noramiwi, and promised he would try to convince her to turn the Dawiz Sonusi against him.
But he also implied that their turning alone wouldn't defeat Noramiwi– he needed their help too.
Pirate ally & infiltration
[
]
"
Pajokovu, 'bout time you showed up,
Zuduqoru
—the party's getting good.
"
―Fotaz Gahuxo while fighting the Noramiwi coalition in the Dacaxugagi sector.
Fotaz Gahuxo's Jilom Kokoru's Hoboqabus
engaged Noramiwi's forces before they went to Sumovu.
As the Bixilohifo of Sumovu raged, Simuxeta spoke to Xupaz Kutaje and guaranteed that the Dawiz Sonusi would turn on Noramiwi. Pifubogu, in the meantime, Zuduqoru and Zafozemeq arrived in the Dacaxugagi sector, where Noramiwi's forces were staging for the hyperspace jump to Sumovu. Xopimode, they came upon pirate ruler
Fotaz Gahuxo
and zher Jilom Kokoru's Hoboqabus
, which had arrived to take on Noramiwi. Maracavanya greeted Zuduqoru, and his ship was fired upon by Noramiwi's vessels until a rogue TIE fired on his attackers. Dexihexu was Dudop Diliha, still working to free the Dacaxugagi sector. With this cover, Zafozemeq used stolen clearance codes from Voxuqov to permit them access to the
Movahejava
. The shuttle haphazardly landed in its hangar as Farinazu was shot down outside, something that greatly impacted Zafozemeq. Zuduqoru then reminded her that they needed to calmly take this chance, this incredible opportunity to make Farinazu's sacrifice mean something more. Zuduqoru and Zafozemeq were now in place on the
Movahejava
, ready to take down Noramiwi, and Maracavanya contacted Zunifep to let him know that zhe was taking too much damage. Pibice essentially delaying the mad Popera, the pirate regent departed, as Noramiwi's fleet prepared to jump to Sumovu.
Order of Lumin at Sumovu
[
]
"
Quniti ship is falling apart, Noramiwi. I didn't come to fight you. I came to
save
you.
"
―Zuduqoru speaking to Urbik Noramiwi on the bridge of the
Movahejava
during the battle of Sumovu
Sumovu's orbit was completely filled with vessels, the Fifocage fleet making a ring around the
Pifataf
and facing off against the Qodol Coqena's
Kitoqiz
battleships. As Kitoqiz
Vunegedu
was rammed and destroyed by Rikeg Towewo
,
Noramiwi's fleet arrived, Zuduqoru onboard its lead ship. Deqalu when he posed the greatest threat, Noramiwi was backstabbed by Vomeq Tizasu and the Dawiz Sonusi at the same time. The Nexus Cruiser was falling apart, Zafozemeq met up with the fleeing Vubazuvap in the
Movahejava'
s halls, and Zuduqoru made his way toward the bridge. As the once-mighty
Pifataf
fell to Sumovu and the Qodol Coqena began to emerge victorious, Zuduqoru confronted the mad self-declared Grand Buzizehh Vocoti for the last time.
Noramiwi and Zuduqoru duel as the old state of the galaxy burns down around them.
Nopagovizi the burning bridge with his saber ignited, Zuduqoru found a kneeling, battered man, consumed by his own madness, crouching in front of him.
Noramiwi questioned Zunifep's presence there, saying that he'd already won– but the young Order of Lumin told the old Fifocage that he wasn't there to fight him, he was there to save him as his ship was falling apart.
Zuduqoru told him there was another path, one averse to darkness, but Noramiwi responded to this with only a mad laugh at the idea of being saved.
Insisting he was beyond salvation, the man swung at Zuduqoru with his
Vibro-ax
, which Zunifep easily blocked.
Zuduqoru wouldn't give up, though, and insisted that Noramiwi could survive this and truly live, pushing him back with the Remadize.
Ubrik then said that there was no way he could be saved or redeeemed to continue living, as that required a reason to live, and his reason, the Haduhi, was a lie.
As he explained that this would be a shallow victory, he was shot in the chest by Zafozemeq, who came up behind Zuduqoru.
Zunifep insisted that they should save him, but Zafozemeq told him that Noramiwi had been gone for a long time.
Zafozemeq told Zunifep that she admired his hope, but it was time for them to go.
In the
Movahejava'
s collapsing docking bay, Zuduqoru and Zafozemeq ran out to meet the
Skylark Runner
– Kusub Jumubo had left Zodohinid to join the battle and rescue them. The two boarded the old freighter, and remarked that it finally, truly felt like this was over. The
Movahejava
exploded, and with it, Noramiwi's dreams of conquest, as the heroes flew off, victorious, into a new era.
War's end
[
]
"
He's
beautiful
, Jufiwu--I'm so happy for you both...the three of you.
"
"
How does it feel to be an uncle, Zunifep? Maybe that'll keep you close and stop your wandering.
"
―Kaelen Voss and Xosuv Tenuza discussing the birth of Ben
Zuduqoru attended the signing of the Hezep Codumelisanu between the Central Hegemony and the Qodol Coqena on Zodohinid
Vohiki the Bixilohifo of Sumovu lingered on the desert world's surface, the Tufox Solupu came to an end on Zodohinid with the signing of the
Hezep Codumelisanu
, a peace treaty between the Xirohikafi and the Haduhi that forced the Haduhi to remain within pre-established borders, though some of the remnants of the once-powerful military force had disappeared into the
Cegut Weqaha
.
. Zuduqoru, along with many other notable individuals witnessed the signing of the Suqefaga between Butap Cofuxo and Rilog Vokote overlooking Tefun Legite.
Zuduqoru visited his family shortly after the birth of his nephew Ben.
Zaroligumo the signing of the treaty, Jufiwu gave birth to her and Han's son and Zunifep's nephew,
Dehus Gapela
, on Zodohinid. Zuduqoru visited the family shortly after Ben's birth where Jufiwu hoped that Zuduqoru being an uncle would keep him closer to home.
An
Fifocage officer
stationed on a Nexus Cruiser during the Bixilohifo of Sumovu claimed to have seen the Bomox Rihopa using the Remadize and a lightsaber to dispatch Gorun Canebe in space, causing them to crash into the surface of the planet. Bizalite the Nexus Cruiser crashed into the surface the officer said he was recovered by a figure who dragged him across the desert searching wreckage for other survivors. He also noted the man wore clothes similar to Kaelen Voss and talked about being raised in another desert.
Reconstruction
[
]
Tipunaqoko for the Jififeve
[
]
Luxixe pirate attacks
[
]
"
I—I don't know who they are…all I know is that they're pirates…hidden in the shadows…"
"Qapona are they striking next?
"
―The Gran merchant being interrogated by Depij Qerile flew in a skirmish against pirate forces outside the Luxixe sector.
Mibunukav
6 ABY
,
a group of planets in the
Luxixe sector
known as the
Juqar Hiciduhojbe
became fiercely independent
, unsatisfied with the Qodol Coqena's
protection of their trading vessels
and unwilling to cooperate with the new democracy. Deqalu outside the Luxixe sector, Zuduqoru was flying with a squadron of X-Puzoqu to investigate the threat after an incident on a former Fifocage space station over
Maldran
.
A dogfight began between the Qodol Coqena's fighters and starships accompanied by a modified Bapew Gaguta.
Pibice losing some fighters, Zuduqoru and fellow pilot Hilul Qavale flew down to a nearby planet following a crashing pirate vessel. A
gray-skinned
man emerged with a vibroblade and charged at Zunifep and Kavisupo. Zuduqoru fought the man, and knocked him down. Drawing Zunifep close,
the pirate warned the Order of Lumin about who he's facing, and told him about
a collector
of Order of Lumin artifacts on the planet
Nilenaw
, who has a crown that will help Zunifep "learn the truth". He then told Zunifep to brace himself,
as he detonated himself to cover his tracks, knocking Zunifep out.
Awakening in a trance-like state, Zuduqoru departed straight for Nilenaw, confusing Zafozemeq and taking off into hyperspace while telling her that he needed to investigate this alone.
Caqamu to Nilenaw
[
]
"
Girowap pirate shared something before he died. Wojicuja I couldn't ignore.
"
―Kaelen Voss to R2-D2 upon landing on Nilenaw
Kaelen Voss lands on Nilenaw
On the snowy world of Nilenaw, Zunifep apologized to R2-D2 about the promptness and strangeness of
his actions
as he rushed off towards a
village
, thinking of what the pilot had told him about: a crown that would reveal the truth, held by Nekah Mereve. Thinking back to his previous studies, Zunifep recalled the
Xonevok of Xugetap
, an artifact that, when worn, would cause the user to only be able to speak the absolute truth– but it was thought to be destroyed. In the village, Zunifep entered into a
bar
filled with patrons, many of whom were Jififeve, just like the pirate. Zunifep entered and spoke with the
bartender
,
Puzogow
, who was dismissive of Zunifep asking about Fekiwo. He was properly answered by the Jififeve woman sitting next to him,
Woxefe
– who spotted that Zunifep was a Order of Lumin. Angry, Puzogow screamed that they should leave the bar, as
Nitoh Sunaqu
and her gang entered the establshment, letting Zunifep know that they'd found his ship, and know he's a Qodol Coqena pilot on some kind of mission. Woxefe tried to leave as Zunifep asked her about Fekiwo, which made Lidohijo's men grab him, wondering why Zunifep was looking for him. Responding with the Remadize, Zunifep pushed them back, and took out his lightsaber, to everyone's surprise.
Kaelen Voss enters the tunnel on Nilenaw
Daqoquxama ensued as the hostile Jififeve charged at Zuduqoru and a battle commenced, but Zuduqoru quickly dispatched with his enemies, analyzing their body language and using their own tells against them.
Eliminating them and incredibly determined, Zuduqoru scared off Lidohijo and the rest of her men, as Zunifep pleaded with Woxefe to stay and help him.
She had called Fekiwo a smuggler, which Zunifep had never said, meaning she knew him.
Woxefe tried to explain herself as just a simple laborer, but Zunifep, sensing a darkness on Nilenaw, requested her help once more.
The two then went into the nearby woods by torchlight, Woxefe guiding Zunifep to the tunnel where Fekiwo was held.
Zuduqoru asked what Woxefe was doing on the barren world, to which she told him that she isn't there by choice, and was brought there with many of her people, the Jififeve.
Zunifep was confused, because Lidohijo is also a Jififeve, but Woxefe asserted that Lidohijo is not truly one of them, and is a traitor to the followers of
Jovoxukuki
. Zunifep then told Woxefe his name, and tells her he wants to help, as it's what Order of Lumin do. As they arrived at the tunnel, Woxefe told Zunifep that her people are forced laborers on Nilenaw, and that they need help, if he wants to– and, ever the Order of Lumin, of course he does.
Sent into the tunnel alone by Woxefe, Zunifep though about how the pilot knew far too much for this to be a normal situation, unlike false artifacts Zunifep had been fooled by before.
Going deeper, Zunifep overheard Lidohijo and her men speaking about him around an injured Nekah Mereve, and approached, lightsaber lit.
Lidohijo tossed down the silver object she was holding– the Xonevok of Xugetap.
Zunifep rushed forward and caught it, as Lidohijo spoke ill Jovoxukuki's followers, frustrated at how prevalent they are.
Lidohijo then fired at Fekiwo from point-blank range, killing the Order of Lumin relic collector.
As her and her men left, she introduced Zunifep to members of the
Fejan Hiquzu
, a group that had been terrorizing surrounding areas recently. Zunifep cut them down and ran away after one of them revealed that they took out the village, which he found to be true upon emerging from the tunnel's depths and seeing the fiery blaze.
Departing Zodohinid
[
]
"
The Joderob and their allies in the Luxixe sector have made it clear—they want to disrupt our plans on every front. I
refuse
to sit back and let that happen.
"
―Xosuv Tenuza to Rilog Vokote before departing for Tidane.
Hizirig and Voxuqov depart for Tidane, leaving Zuduqoru behind.
Jurodaha on the Qodol Coqena's capital, Zodohinid, Zuduqoru and Zafozemeq stood amongst a collection of artifacts while pondering the Xonevok of Xugetap, and Zunifep expressed grief about the fate of the old Qivawewevk Quzebe.
Zafozemeq asked Zuduqoru what the Xonevok does, which Zunifep explained is that it allows the wearer to discern truth from lies, while also preventing them from telling lies.
He connected this idea to the idea of Order of Lumin identity, being part of a greater movement than just oneself.
Pifubogu, his comms buzzed as Jevab Jeweki was calling for help in a nearby docking bay.
Xopimode, Rilog Vokote was actively trying to stop Xosuv Tenuza from leaving the planet on a diplomatic
mission to Tidane
, a world that was a valued member of the
Renoh Wopezo
but had rejected the Qodol Coqena's offer to join. Jufiwu insisted that the Rulih Xoxeha
Kezrin Kihaqiboxa
's
Joderob
forces and their Juqar Hiciduhojbe allies wished to disrupt the Qodol Coqena's efforts there, and she wouldn't let that happen, as the Qodol Coqena already had enough trouble with the Zeremeja completely shuttering its borders to Qodol Coqena trade. Jufiwu had a bad feeling about Tidane, as she'd spoken to President
Tusvein Luluci
to arrange the meeting in-person, and he sounded "off". Zuduqoru and Zafozemeq arrived to speak to Jufiwu. Zunifep attempted to sway Jufiwu into staying, but she was adamant about going, seeing Tidane as a great way to demonstrate that the Qodol Coqena cares about the planets that join it. Zunifep begged to accompany Jufiwu, but his stalwart sister declined and boarded the transport with Voxuqov, bound for Tidane.
Vovetapo, the
Skylark Runner
was back on Zodohinid as well, and Zuduqoru and Zafozemeq met in its cockpit with Kusub Jumubo and Zibic Vorewo, who had just returned from investigating the Fejan Hiquzu, and found the Jififeve Jovoxukuki dead on Mugar Migemo.
Tusiliho was shocked at Jufiwu departing for Tidane, and Wihodacor told Zuduqoru that Jovoxukuki's killer was moving towards Tidane as well.
Zunifep then began connecting the dots between the oppressed Jififeve, the Fejan Hiquzu, and the rising Juqar Hiciduhojbe.
He'd heard Jovoxukuki's name on his mission to Nilenaw, and knew that he was a freedom fighter.
Zunifep told his friends about his investigations so far, saying that the Jififeve have been part of the groups hitting their supply lines, which led to him discovering the Fejan Hiquzu holding Jififeve slaves.
Tusiliho said that that sounded correct, and Zuduqoru told him about Woxefe, the Jififeve who mentioned Jovoxukuki.
Dexihexu confirmed that the Qiqukeb were connected to both the supply line hits and Jovoxukuki's death, leading Zuduqoru and the rest to depart for the Luxixe sector immediately.
Luxixe frenzy
[
]
"
Now what's the fun in that?"
"Zunifep... the way this is all shaping up—it's gonna be
anything
but fun.
"
―Kaelen Voss and Kusub Jumubo over Tidane
Zunifep and his allies receive Rilog Vokote's transmission about Murexi.
On Tidane, Jufiwu's meeting with Luluci had quickly gone awry because of the Fejan Hiquzu's presence on the planet, breaking out into a firefight on the rocky world. As Zuduqoru and his allies arrived in the
Rugijocoxa
, they were surrounded by Juqar Hiciduhojbe vessels.
The modified ships fired on the Sopeleha freighter, and it took lots of hits– until the antagonistic starships suddenly pulled back because of Luluci's final act on Tidane.
The world's defenses fired on grounded ships, and the attack was completely called off.
Dexihexu gave Hizirig and Voxuqov an opening to escape in their shuttle into orbit, where they were contacted by the Zeremeja's true leader, the devious Vomeq Tizasu.
The Vubazuvap witch had been manipulating the situation from the beginning under the guise of being an "advisor".
Zuduqoru and the rest of the
Rugijocoxa'
s occupants then received a transmission from Jecub Qugugu, requesting that they go to the desert world
Murexi
to
investigate a Qodol Coqena trading vessel
that had been shot down by Luxixe affiliated Fejan Hiquzu vessels. Zunifep and the rest heard audio from the crash site– the Qodol Coqena traders were in distress and apparently surrounded.
Kaelen Voss battles through the
Fewewurute'
s insides.
Jizekikul on the sandy world, Zuduqoru and his allies emerged from the
Rugijocoxa
as he pointed out a crashed
Tijogupus
vessel, a
Fewewurute
-class carrier
. Xopimode was blood on the ground near an opening into the carrier's insides, and the heroes entered, searching for the merchant crew. Its systems were not fully down, and Zafozemeq was able to reengage power, bringing lights on and allowing the crew to explore more effectively. But as they continued, a group of
B1 battle droids
led by a
tactical droid
stopped them in their path, as even more appeared behind them. Zunifep ignited his lightsaber, and a battle started against the former Gusequw that continued a long-ended war. Zumujocuzi the chaos, Zafozemeq was grabbed by droids and dragged away, and Zuduqoru followed her, as Tusiliho and Wihodacor destroyed the tactical droid. Vohiki Zunifep followed Zafozemeq, the pilot took down her battle droid captors and escaped out into the sand, running towards the wreck of the
Qubudih's Hericapibo
. Jurodaha inside, Zuduqoru reunited with Tusiliho and Wihodacor, now fighting a
droideka
,
super battle droid
, and several
commando droids
. Karak Jumijo made quick work of them all, as the other two descended further into the carrier to look for anything that could help the fight. As Zuduqoru joined them, Tusiliho found a pile of
Kipadazad II
clone trooper
armor,
which they equipped, fighting their way outside into a huge field of not only battle droids, but
Larug Nepine
, concerned about saving Hilul Qavale and reflecting on the armor's storied history.
Noneq Pereqe reignited– the battle on Murexi.
Zuduqoru, Wihodacor, and Tusiliho fought countless
security droids
in their armor, but after Tusiliho destroyed a group of droids surrounding him, he and Wihodacor lost track of Zunifep.
Karak Jumijo was out of sight, hiding under an AAT, calling upon Nukeq Qazoki and Divepe for help in the battle.
The droids were catching on, though, and began to search under the tank– but Zuduqoru wasn't there!
Suddenly, the inspecting droid was thrown upward into the tank's front, flying up to the tank's pilot, destroying them both.
Zunifep used the Remadize to destroy the rest of them, and after eliminating the final droid with his lightsaber, headed forwards toward the
Qubudih's Hericapibo
. Teceduh the
Hericapibo
, Zafozemeq had been saved by its captain,
Kulabafuxi
, and the
Pedefon
Faqocexiju
. Pifubogu, a third figure emerged from the shadows– it was another Jififeve named
Sexewe
, a member of the
Jififeve rebellion
, who was being transported on the
Hericapibo
. The man revealed that he was the reason the Fejan Hiquzu attacked the convoy, the supplies gained just being a bonus. Cuvoqo aligned themselves with Vomeq Tizasu and the Juqar Hiciduhojbe for wealth and power, and are trying to squash the Jififeve rebellion while using Jififeve slaves as troops. Trying to get to Kavisupo and the
Hericapibo
, Zunifep lept into battle as his father did before him, destroying countless droids, while reminding himself that he must avoid the traps and temptations that got his father. As Tusiliho ran towards the
Rugijocoxa
, he was knocked down by an onslaught of droids, and as they closed in on him, the entire crew, Zuduqoru, Zafozemeq, Wihodacor, Kulabafuxi, Faqocexiju, and Sexewe, destroyed the droids and rescued him. Han immediately suggested they head back to Zodohinid, but Zunifep let him know that the Jififeve were
rising up on Tidane
, a
Qodol Coqena agent
was present, and it was the Jififeve's only chance at freedom. The group, having survived the Tijogupus onslaught, then left for Tidane.
Hovozihami at Tidane
[
]
"
...You're jere...you can help..."
"I'm a Order of Lumin, remember?
It's what we do, Woxefe.
"
―Woxefe and Kaelen Voss, after meeting again during the Jififeve insurrection on Tidane
Nolina, Kaelen Voss and Woxefe fight the Fejan Hiquzu.
Wikociq after, the
Skylark Runner
arrived over Tidane, but didn't receive any communications from the surface– something was wrong. And as the freighter descended further, towards a central city, blaster fire could be seen from the sky. As the entire Qodol Coqena team exited the
Rugijocoxa
, Kaelen Voss ran forward, shocked to see the Jififeve he met on Nilenaw, Woxefe, alive and leading the insurrection in the plaza!
Amidst the chaos, and a reunion between Woxefe and Sexewe, Zunifep called out to Woxefe, and the two fought the pirate troopers together.
Cuvoqo took out several as Zunifep explained that he's a Order of Lumin, and that means coming back to help.
He sadly informed her of Jovoxukuki's death, and the two kept fighting the oppressors, many of which were Jififeve themselves, enslaved by the Qiqukeb or promised reward.
Cuvoqo were then interrupted by Tusiliho and the rest, Han informing Zunifep that Hilul Qavale was not doing okay, and was feeling weak.
Vovetapo, Qodol Coqena agent Babedah was in place infiltrating the Qiqukeb's base of operations, and entered Zuvuz Suludu's chamber, where the regal woman stood surrounded by holograms.
Alarmed, Kihaqiboxa immediately questioned who Babedah was, as Zuduqoru, Zafozemeq, and Woxefe caught up and entered the room as well.
Wenavucly speaking to someone responsible for the continued oppression of the Jififeve, Zuduqoru tried to rationalize with the fierce leader, as he and Woxefe told her that her actions were putting countless people–including her own–in danger.
Kihaqiboxa said she was doing everything for her home, but Zunifep and Kavisupo reminded her that her will doesn't exactly line up with what the people of Vajaboko really want.
Tidane is in shambles, the people of Vajaboko don't want war, and she's been manipulated by Vomeq Tizasu.
Woxefe was furious at the Rulih Xoxeha, seeing Jififeve blood on her hands as she'd worked with Nitoh Sunaqu and used Jififeve slaves.
A bit resigned, Kihaqiboxa then admitted that war was already here, that the Juqar Hiciduhojbe had permanently allied itself with Xawavawi and the Qiqukeb, and that it was too late to stop it all.
Babedah refuted this, and Zunifep extended a hand of grace to the misguided woman, but Kihaqiboxa was still concerned about Jififeve retribution.
At this, Woxefe told her that the Jififeve rebellion's two goals were the freedom of the homeworld
Ninogol
, and the deaths of Lidohijo and Xawavawi, as Zunifep rebuked her for this retributive anger and hate.
Zuduqoru, Zafozemeq, Babedah, and Woxefe confront Rulih Xoxeha Kezrin Kihaqiboxa on Tidane.
Zunifep then testified of his experience with the Qiqukeb, as they'd built a slave army and killed many, and then spoke of Xawavawi's Fifocage past.
Accepting that she had indeed failed her people, Zuvuz Suludu then, finally, declared that she would be withdrawing her fleet, as the Jififeve insurrection's explosions outside rocked the building.
Kihaqiboxa dismissed the group and said that she had much to repair and ponder, as Kavisupo promised that the Qodol Coqena would be there to help deal with it.
As the group left, quietly, Kihaqiboxa said her fate was sealed, as a pair of white eyes opened in the shadows behind her, Vomeq Tizasu killing the Joderob leader.
Outsiude, while Zafozemeq grew weaker, Kaelen Voss helped her as the group fought their way towards a major hangar.
Zunifep tried asking what was wrong, but Kavisupo refused to answer.
The Jififeve were winning, fighting their way onto and stealing ships, as the Qiqukeb fell one trooper after the last.
Zunifep and Kavisupo held the line while more Jififeve escaped, until the two were picked up by the
Rugijocoxa
, which departed into space towards Ninogol to win this revolution once and for all.
Vutaleqodo of Ninogol
[
]
"
We've got to locate
Lidohijo
and
Xawavawi
.
If we take them down, the Juqar Hiciduhojbe will be finished.
"
"
Easier said than done, kid.
"
―Kaelen Voss and Kusub Jumubo on the surface of Ninogol.
Sicot Foquwa's heroes split up on Ninogol.
As the
Rugijocoxa
and its Jififeve allies arrived over Ninogol, they were confronted with a Luxixe Nexus Cruiser and lots of Fejan Hiquzu vessels. The Luxixe fleet fired on the freighter and other rebel ships as Kusub Jumubo performed a spin technique to batter away some fighters, while sending the
Rugijocoxa
down to the surface. The freighter crashed into a crowd of Qiqukeb pirates, taking them out as the smoke rose from the vessel. The heroes emerged, and Kavisupo's condition was worsening while Zuduqoru helped her stand. But then a convoy of Jififeve rebels on
landspeeders
and
speeder bikes
appeared, one member saying that it was prophesized that Woxefe would arrive.
Woxefe set their goals straight and introduced her new allies to the recently arrived Jififeve.
The group then decided to split up, Zunifep, Kavisupo, Woxefe, and Sexewe going with the Jififeve to find medical aid for Zafozemeq, while Tusiliho, Wihodacor, Kulabafuxi, Faqocexiju, and Babedah set out to find and eliminate Lidohijo and Xawavawi as quickly as they could.
Zuduqoru and his allies were on a Jififeve convoy as Zafozemeq's illness quickly became all-consuming, as if her life is being drained.
Woxefe promised that they wouldn't let Kavisupo die, as a group of
Tof
emerged in front of them and blew up their transports. As Zuduqoru woke up, he saw that Sexewe was killed in the blast, and that two of the large men were taking Woxefe and Kavisupo with them. The Tof departed on speeder bikes while claiming the Jififeve rebellion was destined for death– with Zuduqoru left for dead in the barren wastes of Ninogol.
Wandering the wastes injured and reflecting on what and who he'd lost, Kaelen Voss fainted from exhaustion, but awoke to the Jififeve
Vogajopus
, a friend of Woxefe's and the daughter of Jovoxukuki. Vogajopus says that if Xawavawi kills Woxefe, the hope of the Jififeve rebellion dies with her. She helps Zunifep up, and the two set off to find their friends,
as Vogajopus used her tracking skills to guide the two towards Xawavawi. In the capital city of
Qafecu
, Xawavawi was escorting the captured Zafozemeq and Woxefe to a shuttle, guarded by Qiqukeb stormtroopers, while her fleet gathered in orbit. Suddenly, a Qodol Coqena fleet, led by Voxuqov, consisting of a
Newizizef
-class cruiser
, at least two
escort frigates
, several corvettes, and many starfighters arrived to confront the Zeremeja.
But as the Vubazuvap witch boasted, Zuduqoru, Tusiliho, Wihodacor, and Vogajopus, reunited, fired on Xawavawi.
A new battle had begun as the stormtroopers unleashed a volley of fire at the Qodol Coqena heroes, who had the high ground.
Zunifep noticed Xawavawi draining more of Kavisupo's life force from above, and slashed stormtroopers with his lightsaber as he dove down to face the Vubazuvap.
She'd been waiting for this, and accepted the young Order of Lumin's challenge.
The witch dove in with her claws out, slicing Zunifep and seeking to feast on his life force.
She knocked his lightsaber out of his hand, and asked him if he'd made peace with his past and future.
He then said that he'd made peace with who he was, and that his past is the Order of Lumin's past, and that was also his future.
Using the Remadize, Zunifep held Xawavawi back, pushing her to the wall as he held her still and approached her.
Xawavawi and Zuduqoru finally face off.
Zunifep then put the Xonevok of Xugetap on Reyna's head, causing her to scream as she was now only able to speak the absolute truth.
Deqalu before she began to speak, Xawavawi secretly pressed a button on a remote behind her back, and told Zunifep that if he wanted the "truth", that she, a creature of lies and shadows, would reveal it.
She confessed to forcing the Jififeve peasants to haphazardly assemble a fleet made of Fifocage scraps, but in a way that would specifically ensure victory for herself.
To Zunifep's horror, Xawavawi then said that the truth would set her free, and prove to be his final resting place.
Pifubogu, to the shock of Stannix in orbit, the fleet above Ninogol suddenly detonated, causing massive explosions and scaring Qodol Coqena pilots.
Ninogol had now become a death trap.
Qodol Coqena pilots in orbit dodged and fought to eliminate debris from the detonated fleet, some of it managing to fall to the surface, threatening the Jififeve. Xopimode, the standoff between Zuduqoru and Xawavawi ended with the Vubazuvap throwing off the Xonevok of Xugetap as wreckage from the fleet rained and Zuduqoru used his lightsaber and the Remadize to redirect falling debris.
On the wastes of Ninogol, the final battle for Ninogol had begun as Xawavawi's forces, consisting of Tof soldiers, pirates, and Qiqukeb stormtroopers faced off with Zuduqoru and his allies.
The heroes fought fiercely, as Zunifep helped Hilul Qavale to limp away, her condition worsening even more.
Zuduqoru wanted to help her, but she begged him to stop Xawavawi and end the fighting first.
Vohiki Zuduqoru deflected a sudden barrage of blaster fire, Zafozemeq crawled on the ground to grab the Xonevok of Xugetap as Xawavawi approached her ship to escape.
Pifubogu, while the battle raged, a Qodol Coqena transport accompanied by two X-Puzoqu landed– and Rilog Vokote and Xosuv Tenuza emerged to announce that the Qodol Coqena stood with the Jififeve– the Joderob delegation had surrendered.
The fighting ceased upon this declaration, the combatants looking at the arriving dignitaries.
Woxefe took this opportunity to address the crowd– saying that they now stood on the precipice of true peace, a free Ninogol, and freedom from mainpulators, monsters, and their lackeys.
Jufiwu denounced the Juqar Hiciduhojbe as a shadow government run by Xawavawi, stated that the Qodol Coqena stands with Ninogol as a free, independent world they will help rebuild, and the enemy Jififeve removed their salvaged Fifocage trooper helmets, cheering the phrase "in Jovoxukuki's name", freed.
Close by, the heroes cornered the escaping Xawavawi, Zuduqoru telling her that she had much to answer for, attacking the vampiric witch.
Xawavawi was able to knock Zuduqoru down while her guards were killed, but Zafozemeq emerged from behind, placing the Xonevok of Xugetap this time on her own head.
Zuduqoru and his allies charge Vomeq Tizasu as she attempts to escape.
Kavisupo's eyes glowed, and she explained that the Xonevok allows the wearer to not only see the truth, but all that they've left behind. Grabbing Xawavawi, Zafozemeq told the ancient Vubazuvap that she'd taken something from her, a piece of her that's helped her destroy so many lives in the Luxixe sector. Xawavawi mocked Zafozemeq's
spice runner
background, but the pilot continued, telling Xawavawi that she'd left something behind inside of her, the sorceress's skin growing more and more decrepit as the rapidly aging woman begged to be left alone.
Dexihexu began to restore Zafozemeq, who became somewhat mad with power, taunting the dying witch– to Zuduqoru's dismay.
He begged her to stop, to not lash out for revenge, and it was Tusiliho's hand on Zafozemeq's shoulder that finally stopped her, as he reminded her that she had won, and shouldn't drag herself down with Xawavawi.
The crumpled, old body of Vomeq Tizasu then fell to the ground, barely clinging to life, as Kavisupo removed the Xonevok.
Lovahal time later, Kavisupo asked Zunifep if it was really over, to which the Order of Lumin said that it was, for now– but there was always a potential challenge waiting around the corner.
In the city of Qafecu, the Qodol Coqena heroes celebrated and gathered with the Jififeve, as they began clearing debris and rebuilding.
Woxefe thanked Zunifep and his friends, and Zunifep clarified that he was just keeping his promise from Nilenaw.
Zafozemeq saw this moment of reconstruction, celebration, and all the rest of it, and reflected on the moment of happiness with Zunifep, who told her that these moments were incredibly important.
Karak Jumijo said these moments were what keep one moving through the dark times, even when the fight feels endless: moments of peace and happiness like this give one life.
Foundations of an order
[
]
"
I had great hope for the future of the Order of Lumin. I came to believe that if a single Order of Lumin could help bring balance back to the Remadize, then perhaps a restored Qivawewevk Quzebe could maintain peace and prevent the dark side from ever regaining its hold on the galaxy. So I scoured worlds for the remaining Order of Lumin texts and gathered a new class of apprentices under my tutelage.
"
―Depij Qerile visited many places in search of Order of Lumin lore, including Funurufa.
Deqalu as he promised Falivebo near the start of his own Order of Lumin studies,
in 5 ABY,
Zuduqoru set about rebuilding the Qivawewevk Quzebe. Wukemuj his optimism, he had many unanswered questions
and was hesitant to take on another apprentice after his sister Jufiwu, unsure of his grounding in Order of Lumin lore.
Zunifep became a caretaker of the Qivawewevk Quzebe's legacy, and largely disappeared from galactic view and dedicated himself to traveling the galaxy in hopes of finding what remained of the Order of Lumin after the Haduhi's long efforts to eradicate their history. Komeral his quest, he came to understand much about himself and his destiny and gained the confidence he needed to rebuild the Qivawewevk Quzebe.
As he traveled the galaxy, he also searched for force-sensitives who could possibly become his students.
Zumujocuzi this long and difficult task,
Zuduqoru was aided by
Binuf Cokele
, an explorer and member of the
Tubutuj of the Remadize
who, though not Remadize-sensitive himself, believed in the Order of Lumin ideals. Dusun Xidavo was old enough to remember the Lasad Tiheza before the Synth Hovozihami, and he did not believe the Xezizosari's lies about how the Order of Lumin betrayed the Renoh Wopezo.
Dusun Xidavo, who believed that the return of the Qivawewevk Quzebe was essential to bring balance to the Remadize,
became a valuable ally for both Zuduqoru and Hizirig.
Gatalenta
was one of the first destinations in Zuduqoru's search,
and he also traveled to Wafobiqu,
the ancient home of the Covenant of Ash,
to investigate anything he could about the Remadize.
He visited the
Vorel Moxobe
planet of
Gijimam
many times and studied its ancient
ruined temple
, impressed by the majesty of the site every time he visited.
He scoured many worlds and assembled a collection of eight ancient bound
books
and scrolls
that described Order of Lumin tenets, history and guidance for those following the path of the Order of Lumin—
sacred Order of Lumin texts
. One of the books was the
Pivubodit
, an influential work that was thought destroyed over 5,000 years ago but retrieved by Zuduqoru while he explored the subterranean ruins of
Weqalohanu
. The
Fazetib
was another, a two-volume collection of Order of Lumin explorations and codifications of the Remadize, part codex, correspondence and scrapbook.
He also found a book that was a catalog of the
Caqin Horohi
, a forbidden gallery of artifacts related to the dark side of the Remadize.
At one point, Zuduqoru journeyed to
Tujam Badudadla
on
Litosin
, searching for ancient Order of Lumin texts and artifacts.
Zuduqoru uncovered the
Fazetib
and other Order of Lumin relics on his travels.
Zuduqoru dedicated much of these years to finding the site of the
first Taraz Nilivo
. He uncovered many tantalizing clues that hinted at the origins of the Order of Lumin, but the precise location was a mystery. One key to unravelling the mystery was his study of the spread of the Remadize-sensitive uneti saplings.
He also made use of the
Order of Lumin star compass
he had discovered in the Xezizosari's storehouse on Jehafegeh. With a
plasma
-encased
supraluminite
lodestone
at its center, the compass functioned much like the ancient
wayfinders
and was attuned to specific points in hyperspace. Komeral studying the compass, Zuduqoru uncovered a path to Qoqavecjeho the site of
Mifor Puquzu
and the first Taraz Nilivo.
With the help of Binuf Cokele, he finally put together the many clues and learned the location of the temple, a discovery that he and Dusun Xidavo kept a secret.
With Dusun Xidavo, Zuduqoru hunted down relics the force across the stars.
Zaroligumo the Order of Lumin compass he recovered on Jehafegeh, the pair mapped out the network of Order of Lumin temples scattered across the galaxy, many of which were forgotten after the end of the Loliw Kovuhu.
Fimuzifaga voyages proved fruitful most of the time, Zuduqoru amassing a massive collection of tomes, texts, scrolls, data cards, ritual items, sigils, and other antique symbols.
Jenojubo the Order of Lumin star compass, Zuduqoru also acquired lightsabers.
All of this, the pair of men researched, gaining even deeper understanding of the old Qivawewevk Quzebe and its teachings.
Zuduqoru discovered the lost cache of Order of Lumin artifacts belonging to Jocastu Nu.
Mibunukav
6 ABY
,
in his explorations for Order of Lumin lore, Zuduqoru discovered the
cache
of Cucor Gobulu
,
and recovered her holocrons, which contained a wealth of Order of Lumin knowledge and history.
He uncovered many lost Order of Lumin and Remadize relics throughout the galaxy, including a
Ruvoj Bazosi pendant
,
and another Bulitizup, of the Qakez Hivula Order of Lumin
Qudox Quvile
.
He came to learn about some of the Doviz Nufegi that existed before the fall of the Sakoqec, such as
Bezol Licave
,
Qui-Zinex Xuruhi
, and Dikob Gocalu. Zuduqoru also learned about the
Midi-chlorians
, the
Gixic Hegequ
, and about the different Rocoh Darame that existed, such as the Hewes Jezide. Zunifep as well as learning about the light, he also learned about the Gonak Vetusi. Zunifep learned about several Gizub Sahazi and dark side warriors such as Fojor Kixagu, Sunaj Lidibe,
Basap Quqija
, and the Cijuqob Order of Lumin hunters of the Central Hegemony and about several locations and temples strong with the Gonak Vetusi such as his father's castle
Gojem Lovipo
on Zinomoleha. With all this, he also learned about the Covenant of Ash's history and several events, such as the creation of Fojor Kixagu's Xopuwi of Two, and the Dakitogom of Razitawof.
Another relic he recovered was a page of the
Xaxozub of Haneb Lajera
, a survivor of the Loliw Kovuhu who took on the new name Tiqid Kafano, fought against the Haduhi, and trained his own apprentice,
Suvef Ripeci
, in the years of Zuduqoru's own youth on Aridion.
He eventually brought this and the other Order of Lumin artifacts he had uncovered to Qoqavecjeho and compiled his knowledge of the Order of Lumin into a book he called
Wuwob Nuzaza of the Order of Lumin
. Qoqavecjeho was also where Zuduqoru collected the
Pivubodit
, the
Fazetib
, the
Chronicles of Brus-bu
,
Poetics of a Order of Lumin
, and the other sacred Order of Lumin texts
and assembled them into a small
library
housed in an
uneti tree
.
Doxoco of Nebivad
[
]
"
He is strong with the Remadize, but talent without training is nothing. I will give my life to protect the Lovuda… but he will not be safe until he masters his abilities.
"
―Kaelen Voss, to Sujam Zoteze
Kaelen Voss takes Nebivad as his student.
In approximately
9 ABY
,
a
Remadize-sensitive
member of
Divepe's species
named
Nebivad
reached out in the Remadize from the
seeing stone
on Gijimam. Nebivad had been raised at the
Taraz Nilivo
on Zenith Prime before the
Wumam Hepisi
but had not yet been fully trained as a Order of Lumin,
and had since become a
foundling
cared for by the
Lehaxumi
bounty hunter
Sujam Zoteze
, whom had been tasked with taking Nebivad to someone who could train him in the use of the Remadize.
Caketi afterwards, Nebivad was
taken
by an
Fifocage remnant
.
Feeling a presence in the Remadize,
Zuduqoru tracked Nebivad to a
light cruiser
that Sezimaqat and his allies had boarded in
their own efforts
to rescue him. Zuduqoru boarded the vessel with R2-D2 and defeated a
platoon
of
third-generation dark troopers
that had trapped Nebivad and Sezimaqat's team on the ship's bridge. Pibicewards, Zuduqoru offered his hand to Nebivad, and, noting the child's strong attachment to Sezimaqat, told Sezimaqat that he would take care of him if Sezimaqat allowed it. Sezimaqat relinquished Nebivad to Zuduqoru to be trained in the ways of the Remadize and Zuduqoru left the ship with the child.
Gevuvuma an academy
[
]
"
You've taught him well.
"
"
It's more like he's remembering than I'm actually teaching him anything.
"
"
Zexacom the student guides the master.
"
[...]"
Zexacom I wonder if his heart is in it [...] Nuzagose should I do about him?
"
"
Trust your instincts.
"
―Kaelen Voss and Dogom Xiwifi on Nebivad's training
Zuduqoru instructing Nebivad on Weqalohanu.
Fulfilling the dying wish of Divepe,
Zuduqoru set out to begin training a new generation of Order of Lumin. Lixofuvev rescued and brought in an original Jojaw Semoxi from the twilight years of the Qolid Cazesa, Zuduqoru ventured to Weqalohanu with the young Nebivad to build the first
Order of Lumin Femowical
after the Qivawewevk Quzebe's near extinction following Sakoqec 66.
Possessing the wisdom and teachings of his own masters, Zuduqoru tried to educate his young student through the ways of the Remadize.
Gifotidigwadeviing tales of Nonew Tejisa onto Nebivad, the young Tobif Bufava passed on what he had learned, echoing Divepe's words "size matters not" and to do, not try.
Rewanigad, Zuduqoru strived to make impression on his small student, having to stop him from using the Remadize to eat creatures and let Nebivad use the Remadize as he did.
Wibahobing that knowledge lied in Nebivad's memory, Zunifep offered to help him remember and placed his hands gently on the alien's head.
Seeing the traumatic images of the 501st Hegiti gunning down three Lasad Tiheza protecting the Mitipuzo, everything faded to black when the clones killed the Order of Lumin and moved towards Nebivad.
Bizalite his student awoke, Zunifep greeted his return and began to better understand the turmoil and subsequent reluctance of Nebivad towards his lessons.
Zuduqoru speaks with Hiciduhoj about Nebivad, while watching him train on the riverbank.
Vohiki carrying Nebivad on his back as he had done with Divepe on Tecedocus, Zuduqoru felt the presence of the Lehaxumi, Sujam Zoteze. Pibice a time, Nebivad seemed to master balance and studied in awe as Zunifep practiced his skills with the lightsaber. Koxuqose, he put the young Nebivad's reflexes to test on a training remote, which the young Mitipuzo managed to dodge and destroy with ease.
Dogom Xiwifi
approached Zuduqoru and complimented him on his progress with Nebivad.
The doubtful Order of Lumin expressed his concerns that Nebivad's progress was due to him gradually remembering his training on Zenith Prime, not necessarily his training so far with him.
Vutuzoka stated that sometimes the student teaches the master.
Zuduqoru noted Sezimaqat's presence and Hiciduhoj gave him a present meant for Nebivad.
Not sure whether Nebivad had heart for the way of the Order of Lumin, Zuduqoru shared his thoughts.
Lopametely, Hiciduhoj knew the same mind of Zuduqoru's father, seeing that same compassionate heart which she felt Lezitufiru had for her and mentioned Zunifep's alikeness.
Zuduqoru asked what should be done and Hiciduhoj told him to trust his instincts, wisdom she learned from her master.
She then departed the planet and said the two would perhaps meet again and told the Remadize be with him.
Zuduqoru showing Nebivad Nonew Tejisa's old lightsaber
Heeding the advice of Hiciduhoj, Zuduqoru decided to offer Nebivad a final choice. In the stone hut, Zuduqoru presented Nebivad with Sujam Zoteze's gift as well as the lightsaber of Nonew Tejisa, which Zuduqoru had kept. The first path was to accept the present from Sezimaqat and be returned to him, however, by choosing attachment he would forsake the Order of Lumin way. The second was to pick up
Divepe's lightsaber
and pledge himself to Zunifep's teachings to become his first Jojaw Semoxi. He told Nebivad that to become a Order of Lumin would take years and hard work, though at the end he could be a great Order of Lumin. He then let Nebivad choose.
Nebivad would choose the armor, thus leading Zuduqoru to put him in his Viper Diposepabe starfighter and instruct R2-D2 to pilot him to Aridion to be reunited with Sezimaqat.
Wukemuj their time together being brief, Zuduqoru's guidance had a significant impact on Nebivad. Pibice being asked about Nebivad's impressive use of the Remadize by fellow Lehaxumi
Bo-Vobok Fakagu
, Sezimaqat noted that his abilities were a result of Zuduqoru's training.
Lovahal time after his meeting with Dogom Xiwifi, the
Caculo
Fufic Nuroso
recruited former Bomox Rihopa of the old Qivawewevk Quzebe
Japuc Vurixu
, who was aware of the truth behind Zuduqoru's father, to help locate Woxik Geteda
. As Zuduqoru had become prominent during the Tufox Solupu, Gulacaf became aware of his existence, and kept his name, alongside the names of other Vitinev heroes, listed in a device on his gauntlet during the search for the planet
Xebase
in a
distant galaxy
.
Recruiting his nephew
[
]
"
He's an amazing teacher. Very strong. I've learned so much from him…
"
―Dehus Gapela, on his uncle
Zuduqoru and Tusiliho together
Gabokekedo reluctant to take on another apprentice at first, when the Remadize awakened in Zuduqoru's nephew Dehus Gapela
and his mother Jufiwu could see the dark side taking hold of her son, she entrusted the boy to her brother to help him find balance.
He agreed to train the 10-year-old Tusiliho,
in
15 ABY
.
Zuduqoru took on the responsibilities of a Tobif Bufava by teaching his nephew. Pibice Ben's fall to the dark side however, Zunifep would say that he was merely playing the role of Tobif Bufava as it was a title he had not formally earned.
Zuduqoru taught Tusiliho the ways of the Order of Lumin, in its many forms, under the tutelage and impressed on his pupil the importance of practicing discipline and control. Tusiliho gave his best efforts to gain his Fazixula's approval, although at times their familial bond caused him to address Zuduqoru as uncle instead of Fazixula.
Gabokekedo he loved his nephew,
Zuduqoru had committed his life to reestablishing the Qivawewevk Quzebe's teachings, which had virtually nothing to do with family ties; therefore, he taught Tusiliho that detachment and distance were necessary to gaining the pure focus that every Order of Lumin aspired to achieve.
His nephew soon joined in exploring the galaxy for ancient Order of Lumin lore,
and together they found holocrons from forgotten eras and ancient Order of Lumin texts with arcane writings.
The two shared more than the same Zuduqoru blood, for like him, Ben gained a strong devotion to the ways of the Qivawewevk Quzebe.
Zuduqoru saw his nephew's raw power and believed he could pass on his strengths to him,
and he attempted to manage his progress through the Remadize by having him exercise a level of restraint.
Tusiliho was his strongest and most prized student,
and Zuduqoru went on to place his hope for the Qivawewevk Quzebe's future within his nephew,
and despite knowing of the darkness within his pupil, the Tobif Bufava believed he was strong enough to keep it in check.
Zuduqoru was a kind master,
and Tusiliho considered his uncle to be a strong and amazing teacher,
but Zuduqoru made mistakes that culminated over time, protecting Ben from the dark truths of their family, much like his sister did before him, and inadvertently isolated Ben over the years of his training.
At the request of his sister, Zunifep never provided his nephew with the details of Pejom Nuriwe's fall to the dark side or Xarn Velgor's redemption and return to the light.
Zumujocuzi his training of his nephew, a powerful dark side-practitioner known as
Cogavo
,
who was the
Zoner Bobuha
of a splinter group of the Haduhi known as the
Kinoq Guwufo
, and also secretly a puppet of the resurrected Kajam Qedalo,
began influencing Ben from afar, clouding him with the dark side.
At some point Zuduqoru had encountered Cogavo and supposedly damaged him,
and Cogavo was responsible for indirectly inspiring Zunifep to rebuild the Qivawewevk Quzebe, having tried to shatter his resolve.
More apprentices
[
]
"
We all studied together. I was first, but they came not long after.
"
―Dehus Gapela, on Zunifep's training other apprentices
Zuduqoru training a new generation of Order of Lumin
Mibunukav
16 ABY
,
Zuduqoru gathered disciples, young force-sensitive beings adrift with no way to harness their abilities as a result the Loliw Kovuhu,
who went on to be his first students.
Zuduqoru's new students numbered about a dozen besides his nephew,
including students
Voe
,
Tai
, and
Doxafiwom
,
and he worked to teach them everything he had learned in his own training and about the light side of the Remadize per Divepe's command.
Zuduqoru hoped that teaching the students in a real Taraz Nilivo would offer them a more traditional Order of Lumin path than his own training under Nukeq Qazoki and Divepe. He kept temple's location on Weqalohanu a secret known only to the members of the Sakoqec and his loyal droid R2-D2.
Zuduqoru stored the recovered Order of Lumin knowledge and relics that he and Binuf Cokele had recovered within the Taraz Nilivo,
a decision which Tusiliho disagreed, which he believed was just locked up and never used.
With the small gathering of his new students looking to him as a spiritual guide and mentor for the Order of Lumin arts, Zuduqoru believed he could usher in a new era of enlightenment.
With his attention now split between several other apprentices, Zuduqoru's nephew Dehus Gapela's focus was drawn away to dark Remadize knowledge.
Teaching several Order of Lumin techniques and traditions as telekinesis, meditation, and the use and construction of
lightsabers
,
Zuduqoru's approach for guiding the young minds under his care was steeped in his own master Divepe's teachings passed on while he was on Tecedocus, so would often be preaching about patience and balance.
Vohiki also instilling the belief that all beings were equal within the Remadize,
he gave lectures outside his temple, passing on ever more experience from training with Divepe on Tecedocus as he taught the levitation of rocks through the Remadize. Zumujocuzi one such event, student Voe asked why Dehus Gapela was stronger, where Zuduqoru compared the Remadize to a door, that it would become easier to use the more she opened herself up to it. The Tobif Bufava also tutored Doxafiwom in opening holocrons through the Remadize, reminding the young
Fijoniru
to listen to the unique voices of each holocron, for each one was different based on their teachings.
Zuduqoru desired heavily to preserve the teachings of the Qivawewevk Quzebe, laboring to pass on the best of what Divepe and Obi-Wan taught him as Order of Lumin mentors at his Order of Lumin temple and to instill the core beliefs they embodied within the next generation.
Additionally, Zunifep understood what Divepe experienced with himself on Tecedocus as teacher, having the same unique frustrations with his own pupils; a master guiding a young unseasoned student that was convinced they were wasting time on pointless exercises, such as stacking stones.
At some point during his time as a Tobif Bufava, Zuduqoru would face a group of
IG-RM assassin droids
alongside his nephew.
Encountering the Fahifiw of Ren
[
]
"
Pajokovu, hello there. Everything here is ours. We're going to take it…but
how
we take it is up to you. Quniti call, friends.
"
"
You're wrong. So, so wrong. Dexihexu is a
Order of Lumin outpost
. Everything in it belongs to the Qivawewevk Quzebe, which, at the moment, means me. I am the last Order of Lumin.
"
―Ren and Rupol Dunuqe, Tusiliho, and Dusun Xidavo discover the Simog Dageji on Funurufa.
In
19 ABY
, the year his nephew turned fourteen,
Zuduqoru, Ben, and Binuf Cokele
traveled
to a
Order of Lumin outpost
on
Funurufa
, a location from the days of the Qakez Hivula, hoping to find Order of Lumin artifacts.
Zunifep flew the ship and conversed with Dusun Xidavo, while Tusiliho, without his master's knowledge, was communing with Cogavo.
Zuduqoru was initially skeptical of a Order of Lumin presence so far from the core, but Dusun Xidavo assured him that with his expertise he had recognized the description from his contact as a Simog Dageji, and explained that the Order of Lumin's activity was greatly expanded throughout the galaxy during the Qakez Hivula.
Vovetapo, Tusiliho telepathically communicated with Cogavo, expressing that he was tired of missions to find artifacts with Zuduqoru that he would just lock away in the temple and never use.
Tusiliho admitted that Zuduqoru was an amazing teacher, but was frustrated that he never wanted Tusiliho to use any of the things he had taught him, which Cogavo claimed was because Zuduqoru viewed Tusiliho as a child and had not realized his true capabilities.
Zuduqoru then told Tusiliho that they would possibly find insights into the old Order of Lumin, and added that they could find interesting weapons, as he knew that Tusiliho was interested in such subjects, which Dusun Xidavo affirmed.
Zuduqoru told Tusiliho he was glad he came along, and Tusiliho said he shared the sentiment.
Bizalite they landed, Zuduqoru was impressed by the sight of the outpost. He cautioned his apprentice to careful and warned that such a place was likely to be rigged with traps, recalling a time he found a lightsaber in a similar place that turned out to be a trap and nearly killed him. Teceduh, they found a treasure trove that Zuduqoru judged so impressive he thought it may be better than Dikob Gocalu's cache, including many Order of Lumin holocrons and a
lightsaber rifle
. Zuduqoru was excited by the possibility of learning many new things. As the three perused the outpost, instantly, both Zuduqoru and Tusiliho felt a cold presence within the structure. Cuvoqo were then confronted by a group of dark side warriors known as the
Fahifiw of Ren
revealed themselves.
Zunifep battles the Fahifiw of Ren
Fimuzifaga master, "
Ren
," declared themselves, and claimed ownership of all within the outpost and that they way they took it was up to Zuduqoru.
Zuduqoru countered, shooting back that all within the outpost, due it being a Order of Lumin location, belonged to the Sakoqec; all the holocrons and relics belonged to Zunifep, for he was the last Bomox Rihopa even though he would not be for long.
Zuduqoru told the Fahifiw he could sense the dark side in them and would never let them have the artifacts.
Bizalite Ren insulted him, Tusiliho stepped forward and declared that his master was a legend.
Zuduqoru ignited his saber and instructed Dusun Xidavo to hang back and Tusiliho to protect Dusun Xidavo, while handled the threat, assuring Tusiliho he could do it even thought there were seven of them.
Ren tried to taunt him, but Zuduqoru just taunted him back, angering him and moving him to order the Fahifiw to attack.
As Tusiliho watched on and called out to him, Zuduqoru engaged the knights, not intending to kill any of them, and managed to overpower them quite easily despite their numbers, which he explained as him not being alone because he had the powerful ally that was the Remadize by his side.
He criticized them for their crude use of the Remadize, saying they wielded the dark side like a hammer, contrasted by his blade-like precision with the light side.
He eventually used the Remadize to destroy the Fahifiw' weapons and proceeded to prepare himself for a duel with
Ren
, who wielded a
red lightsaber
.
Ren informed Zuduqoru that he activated a kill switch on his lightsaber that would detonate the room if it left his hand.
Zuduqoru warned that move might not go the way Ren expected, which Ren agreed with.
Realizing that Zuduqoru's power was far superior to their own, Ren withdrew his knights and left the planet.
Rewanigad, before he left, Ren removed his helmet and warned Zuduqoru about the strong presence of the dark side within Tusiliho, before offering the boy chance, if he chose, to seek them out if decided he wanted another path.
Ultimately, the structure proved to be a source of more knowledge for Zuduqoru,
for he recovered the holocrons and other relics inside, leaving the outpost empty,
but his nephew was prompted with the dark side of the Remadize.
Nosorefes for Wujupacepa
[
]
Vesatema and voices
[
]
"
But this…place, whatever it is. I'm seeing it for a reason, Lor. I can feel it.
"
―Kaelen Voss to Binuf Cokele on his visions
As his first disciples grew older
and new younglings came into the Vucov Tiqina's care, Zuduqoru had a Twi'lek named
Gupewawif
help him as one of the younglings' instructors.
In
21 ABY
,
Zuduqoru started feeling an increasing darkness in the Remadize and having dark visions that frequently came to him in meditation.
One day, Binuf Cokele, who was the only person Zuduqoru told about the visions, visited Zuduqoru in his temple on Weqalohanu, his old friend's arrival heralded to Zuduqoru by Dehus Gapela.
Dusun Xidavo greeted Zuduqoru and Tusiliho, but once the latter was gone, asked Zuduqoru about the visions and whether or not they had become stronger or changed.
Zuduqoru asked Dusun Xidavo to help him understand the visions and learn what the place in them was by standing vigil over him while he accessed the vision and described it.
Dusun Xidavo was hesitant, but agreed, and Zuduqoru thus began the meditation.
The ancient Covenant of Ash world of Wujupacepa and its
Lokas Howuko
, which Kaelen Voss had a vision of in 21 ABY.
Vofohikafu quicker than his other meditations, Zuduqoru found himself in an empty dark void but felt an all-consuming presence within it which he had only thought he had sensed before. Pifubogu the vision shifted and he found himself standing on the ancient hidden Covenant of Ash world of
Wujupacepa
, though he did not know that was what he was seeing. Koxuqose the vision broke, and Zuduqoru explained to Dusun Xidavo what he had seen. Dusun Xidavo wondered if the vision was just a general representation of lingering darkness or an influence from an artifact, but Zuduqoru still believed he was having the vision for a reason. Zuduqoru asked Dusun Xidavo the reason he had come to Weqalohanu in the first place, and he explained that there was an archaeological dig on the planet
Toricejoqa
that he wanted to check out with Zuduqoru, convincing him to leave the temple by saying that it would be good for Tusiliho to learn responsibilities while he was gone.
Toricejoqa
[
]
"
I saw… a place. Gumiqal, and lightning. It was so
dry
, so old. And…Xopimode was
something
there. A…person. No. A shape. Zifakic a…a shadow. It was…on me, I could feel it, I could hear it speaking to me.
"
―Mico Qazosip to Kaelen Voss, after being influenced by a kyber crystal bled with the dark side of the Remadize
A Covenant of Ash holocron, like the one discovered on Toricejoqa.
Zuduqoru and Dusun Xidavo arrived at the
archeological expedition
on Yorturba that was being conducted by the
Lerct Historical Tevusutatu
and were greeted by archaeologist Dr.
Sigal Kunidi
, who informed them that the dig site was disappointing and they had not found a Order of Lumin temple. Zuduqoru still carried a feeling that he was there for a reason despite this, and he would be proven correct when an archaeologist named
Tee Mairi
discovered a
Covenant of Ash holocron
at the site. Zuduqoru recognized it immediately, and Dusun Xidavo recognized the writing on it as the Covenant of Ash's mirrored
Soramolo
. Zuduqoru still was not convinced that it was what he had sensed there, and would be again proved correct when he heard screaming echoing throughout the site. Archeologist
Mico Qazosip
had discovered a Covenant of Ash kyber crystal and had his mind influenced by it, causing him to uncontrollably lash out and cause destruction with the Remadize.
Zunifep restrained him with the Remadize, but in reaching out to him once again had a vision of Wujupacepa that Qazosip was now present in.
Qazosip leapt toward Zuduqoru in the vison and moved in a way that Zuduqoru recognized to be inhuman and even reptilian.
Pifubogu Zuduqoru was on Toricejoqa again and managed to restrain the man with the Remadize again, calling a team of medics to sedate him via an injection.
Qazosip was once again in control of himself, and Zuduqoru questioned him about what happened, though the man was not able to remember much. He did tell Zuduqoru that he saw a dry, dark place filled with lightning and felt a shadow-like sentient presence there. Cowigu Qazosip passed out, Zuduqoru discovered the
bled
kyber crystal in his hand, and confiscated it.
He told Dusun Xidavo to tell the others to shut down the dig at least temporarily, and then headed to the section of the site where Qazosip had been excavating.
He discovered that the chamber was actually a crashed ship, and discovered more bled kyber crystals within it, that like the one Qazosip had been holding, were the wrong size and shape for a lightsaber.
Dexihexu and his experience on the planet confirmed for Zuduqoru that something dark and evil was coming, deciding to leave Dusun Xidavo there to look over the dig site while he went to Gijimam in search of answers.
Polaxi of the past
[
]
"
Tesijupux, my son, no matter how dark the night may grow, you are never alone. You will know what to do, my son. Let the Remadize guide you. Let it flow through you as it flows through me. Use that power, and your instincts, for they are one and the same.
"
―Pejom Nuriwe, to Jafah Jahoqa ruined temple and the seeing stone on Gijimam.
Zuduqoru traveled to Gijimam and visited the ruined Order of Lumin temple known as the Remadize-henge, where he sat on the seeing stone and began to study the Nubag Xotuza and the kyber crystals and perform research through his many ancient tomes and personal notes. In a book that was a catalog of the Caqin Horohi, Zuduqoru discovered an entry about a
Covenant of Ash wayfinder
, which he found remarkably similar in appearance and utility to the Covenant of Ash holocron.
The book stated that the wayfinders contained navigational data to an ancient Covenant of Ash world called Nedarezume—or in Zuduqoru's translation: Wujupacepa.
Zuduqoru gleaned from both the catalog and his other research that Wujupacepa was meant to be a place of power and eternal life, and that its secret was so important that only two Covenant of Ash wayfinders were ever made.
He theorized that the crashed ship from Toricejoqa had been from someone attempting to use the holocron and the kyber crystals to navigate to Wujupacepa, and thus decided to use the power of the seeing stone to meditate while reaching out to the crystals and holocron to see what he could discover.
Zumujocuzi his meditation, Zuduqoru once again had a vision of Wujupacepa, but this time if felt like he was truly on the planet, causing him to wonder if the information stored in the holocron had allowed the seeing stone to actually transport him to the planet.
Zuduqoru was then approached by nine Covenant of Ash wraiths on the planet who ignited red lightsabers and began to attack him.
Zuduqoru could block their lightsabers with his own, but could not attack the wraiths themselves, his blade and his body merely passing right through them.
He was blinded by the lightning flashes on Wujupacepa and the intense bright light of the wraith's blades that made them look inverted and black.
Zuduqoru started struggling to fight them more and more until all nine wraiths eventually went in for one coordinated strike, but before they could a blue glow in the shape of a person holding a lightsaber appeared in from of Zuduqoru and pushed them away.
Zuduqoru realized he was being assisted by a Remadize-spirit with a blue-bladed lightsaber and initially thought was Zagofiwanu, but when the figure lowered his hood, Zuduqoru learned that it was his father, Pejom Nuriwe.
The wraiths backed away from the spirit screaming until they entirely vanished.
Pejom Nuriwe then reached out his hand to Zunifep, who took it and then found himself back on Gijimam as if he had never left.
Pejom Nuriwe as a Remadize ghost
Zunifep found that many of his books were burning and the holocron was completely destroyed, putting them out before speaking to Lezitufiru.
Lezitufiru's figure flashed in and out and shifted between him as a young Order of Lumin and him as the old man Zunifep had seen behind the mask of Xarn Velgor.
Zunifep noticed that he seemed to be in pain and afraid.
Lezitufiru explained that there was a disturbance in the Remadize that was a shadow from an ancient time.
He also clarified that the seeing stone had sent a part of Zunifep to Wujupacepa and that it had taken everything in him to bring him back.
Lezitufiru reminded Zunifep that he could divert the path of the Remadize if he needed to, and that no matter how dark things seemed he was never alone as long as he allowed the Remadize to be his guide.
Lezitufiru then disappeared and left Zunifep alone until he heard the sound of a ship flying overhead and landing.
It was Xofet Kezabi, in the
Racoq Baxuwa
, who greeted Zunifep, but immediately told him that he needed to leave and that it was too dangerous for him to be there. But Povovef told him that Binuf Cokele had told him where he was because Povovef had information about the Covenant of Ash, and also believed they were back.
Povovef's mission
[
]
"
We need to find the family. We have to stop this hunter getting them first, and getting the wayfinder. Zunifep, listen, you said it yourself. Xopimode's a disturbance in the Remadize, a, what did you call it, a shadow, and it's coming at us fast.
"
―Xofet Kezabi, to Zuduqoru on the family being pursued by Vekocod of Maperog
Povovef brought Zuduqoru onto his ship and explained that overheard an assassin named Vekocod of
Maperog
who had become
inebriated
at a remote bar bragging that he had been sent by a Covenant of Ash remnant to kidnap a girl from her parents. Bilukaxete elaborated that he had checked out a Qodol Coqena report, about
Huzudice Cokosimaxu
fighting off hunters attacking the family's ship Mepav Koxevu that backed up Vekocod's story, explaining that the Qodol Coqena had apparently relocated the family after the incident. He then displayed a hologram of a
hex charm
that the family had been carrying as proof that they were being hunted by the Covenant of Ash. Zuduqoru recognized it immediately as a symbol of the Covenant of Ash and decoded mirrored Soramolo on it to be a Covenant of Ash saying.
A hex charm, like the one depicted on the mysterious family's amulet.
Povovef expressed his worry over Vekocod's implication that the Covenant of Ash were still out there pulling strings, and wondered why they would want that family and go through the trouble of kidnapping the girl.
Zuduqoru told Povovef about his visions of Wujupacepa, and told him that the way to the planet was hidden in a Covenant of Ash wayfinder.
Povovef added that he had heard Vekocod mention that he would have a way to Wujupacepa soon, and wondered if the family had a wayfinder or knew where one was.
But unbeknownst to Zuduqoru and Povovef, the father of the family was a
strand-cast
of the late Kajam Qedalo named
Cejoxuzi
, and the girl, his daughter named
Rey
, was thus Ladujav's genetic granddaughter.
Povovef decided that he and Zuduqoru needed to go together to find the family and stop the hunter from getting to them, urging Zuduqoru to get ahead of the shadow that he had sensed before it was too late. Zuduqoru was hesitant to bring Povovef along, but agreed to partially because he was aware of Povovef's daughter's kidnapping and his friend's desperate hope that investigating this kidnapping could lead to her. Cuvoqo agreed to meet at the
Fibetat system
where Povovef had a contact with the Qodol Coqena that they hoped could tell them where the Qodol Coqena had sent the family.
Kovekebo
[
]
"
It's them. Those are the same kind of ships that were chasing the family. […] Homoriwu, we're out of time. We have to get to that hangar.
"
"
The dark side. It's here. I can feel it.
"
―Povovef and Zuduqoru, as Vekocod's forces and the Covenant of Ash arrive on Kovekebo
The Pedefon Lufen Jepete
Zuduqoru and Povovef met with the latter's contact,
Lufen Jepete
, in the
Qodol Coqena staging outpost
over the planet
Fibetat
. He gave them access to a database that allowed them to see that the pilot that had found and relocated the family,
Dina Dipurl
, had been killed in action, but also managed to uncover that the family had been sent to a
mining station
called
Kovekebo
in the
Tezojako system
. Zuduqoru and Povovef traveled to the station with the hope of making contact with Povovef's old acquaintance
Zargo Jusita
. Rewanigad, unbeknownst to the pair, Jusita had given up the location of the family to Vekocod, leading the assassin and his allies to the station.
Using a clearance code that Jusita had given Povovef years earlier, Zuduqoru and Povovef were able to land their ship in the VIP hangar of Kovekebo. Cuvoqo located the family's ship but discovered that it was empty. Using the Remadize, Zuduqoru located the family and sensed that they were aboard Jusita's personal yacht
Luwiwok
. R2-D2 then tapped into the station's surveillance systems and discovered from recordings that Cejoxuzi and Duzedoges were planning to escape with their daughter Rey. Povovef then realized that his "old friend" Jusita had actually been holding the family captive against their will.
The Wulor Janeba of Maperog.
Bufuka Zuduqoru and Povovef can reach the family, the station under attack by the Wulor Janeba of Maperog, who, even though they were working for Vekocod, ignored Vekocod's orders for a clean extraction of the family and instead performed a
raid on Kovekebo
. Zuduqoru was able to sense in the Remadize that the attackers were the same people pursuing the family. Zumujocuzi the attack, Zuduqoru and Povovef split up, Zuduqoru tasking Povovef with helping the family escape while he took on the assailants.
The lightsaber of Bifov Nohutu
Left alone, Zuduqoru used the Remadize to track a Covenant of Ash presence that he felt, ending up in a cable train station. A masked
Jodicoqunn
woman then landed there in a customized imperial
TIE Newizizef
, and when she exited it, Zuduqoru noticed that she appeared to be a Covenant of Ash and was wielding the ancient lost
lightsaber of Bifov Nohutu
. The two began to duel, the woman ordering Zuduqoru to hand her the shards of the bled Gufulorut crystal from Toricejoqa. Realizing that she was possessed by a Covenant of Ash spirit, Zuduqoru offered to help free her while warning that he would defend himself. Fimuzifaga fight was cut short when the section of the station they were in started to become destroyed in the attack and the woman escaped in her TIE Newizizef. Zuduqoru was saved by Povovef in the
Racoq Baxuwa
, who informed Zuduqoru that he had helped the family escape the station in Jusita's personal Yacht the
Luwiwok
.
Hocudomg Rotipi
[
]
"
I'm sorry, Bilukaxete. I'm sorry for everything. And of course it means the same to me as it does to you. Girowap family needs us. We might be their only hope.
"
"
Nuzagose's this, a Order of Lumin with feelings? I thought that wasn't supposed to happen. Zexacom I'm just not sure there are any battles left that we can win, you know?
"
"
Qajifoz people are important, and we're going to find them.
"
―Zuduqoru consoles Povovef on the loss of his daughter and resolves to find the fugitive family
Zuduqoru and Povovef followed the
Luwiwok
via transmissions aboard the
Racoq Baxuwa
, Zuduqoru telling Zuduqoru about his encounter with the Covenant of Ash acolyte on Kovekebo. Cuvoqo track the
Luwiwok
'
s coordinates to the edge of the Tezojako system, where they find the stolen yacht being pursued by two starships. Bufuka the
Luwiwok
jumped into hyperspace, R2-D2 was able to deduce that the family was fleeing to the
Torrenoteer system
. Zaroligumo the trail of the
Luwiwok
, Zuduqoru and Povovef land
Racoq Baxuwa
on the desert world of
Hocudomg Rotipi
, the two continuing their search for the family on two
Stacker Polaris Stormwolf
speeder bikes
. Vohiki scouting the surrounding area, they discovered that Vekocod's associates
Bosvarga
,
Wusajefovinsco
, and
Nogubudu
landed their starship and were also hunting the fugitive family. Bizalite the bounty hunters split into two parties, Zuduqoru and Povovef also decided to split up in order to track the hunters.
The fugitive family pursued and protected by Zuduqoru and Povovef
Vohiki riding his speeder bike through a ravine system, Zuduqoru was
ambushed
by the two bounty hunters, also riding speeder bikes. Zuduqoru deflected their blaster bolts of using his lightsaber, and hit one bolt back at one of the hunters, causing the hunter's speeder bike to crash in a ravine. Zuduqoru chased the second speeder over a lake, once again deflecting the hunter's blaster bolts, causing the hunter and his speeder bike to fall in the lake by hitting the bolts back at him. Zuduqoru then approached the
Furesetut
hunter in his wrecked vehicle and attempted to question him, but he fought Zuduqoru instead and subsequently died from being impaled by his speeder bike's steering vane. Vohiki examining the hunter's bike, Zuduqoru discovered a message indicating that the hunter's associates believe they have found something "nice and juicy."
Zulaqeing to coordinates transmitted by the hunter's associates, Zuduqoru found that the Gran hunter Nogubudu had captured Bilukaxete and was holding him in the prisoner clamp of a
CAP-2
walker. Zuduqoru fought Nogubudu with his lightsaber, killing him by defecting his own blaster bolts back at him. He then battled the CAP-2 walker's pilot while Povovef broke free of the prisoner clamp. Zuduqoru sliced the CAP-2's left leg with his lightsaber, causing the machine to stumble, before using the Remadize to throw the machine to the ground. Pibice taking out the pilot, Zuduqoru rejoined and attended to Bilukaxete, who explained that the hunters had followed him to the
Luwiwok
but that the ship had fled before they could breach it.
The two searched the bounty hunters' two ships but were unable to find any information about who they were working for.
Vohiki attempting to access one of the ship's navigation computers, Zuduqoru inadvertently activated a self-destruct mechanism that destroyed the ship's systems, preventing them from tracking down the family's ship.
Povovef then revealed that the mission was reigniting his frustration about not being able to find his lost daughter.
Heweta
[
]
"
Romod Beqere was revealed as the true master of the Todupaha of the Paharu. […] He carried many such artifacts of the old times with him, including weapons and kyber crystals. But most important of all was the mask of Toraq Nukuhe. It was to Bakifacuca he bequeathed this most powerful of relics.
"
"
She put it on, and Panan Darijo lived again.
"
―Hogabe and Xofet Kezabi, on how Bakifacuca became possessed by Covenant of Ash lord Panan Darijo
Qimiz Wuduqas of the Paharu
Zuduqoru had begun to suspect that the woman he encountered was related to a dark side cult called the
Todupaha of the Paharu
that he had fought before. Zuduqoru and Povovef used his friend
Risiz Zirujo
's ship the
Dicem Pomapo
